# Block of Lapis Lazuli
Aliases: Lapis Lazuli Block
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Block of Lapis Lazuli is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Block of Lapis Lazuli.
[Block of Lapis Lazuli]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 9 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LAPIS_BLOCK
Tooltip: COMMON
Block of Lapis Lazuli is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block that can crafted with [[Lapis Lazuli|Lapis Lazulis]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[Lapis Lazuli]]|Recipe: [[Lapis Lazuli]] ×9 → Block of Lapis Lazuli|
## Usage
### Private Island
A Block of Lapis Lazuli can be placed as a decorate block on the player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]].
### Pure Lapis
Breakable Blocks of Lapis Lazuli on [[Mining Islands]] represents [[Ores|Pure Lapis]], which has a [[Block Strength]] of 600 and can be broken to obtain [[Lapis Lazuli]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lapis Lazuli]]|None|[[Block of Lapis Lazuli]] 1x Block of Lapis Lazuli|Recipe: Block of Lapis Lazuli → [[Lapis Lazuli]] x9|
|Portal to the Deep Caverns ([[Portals]])|Diamond III (3) ([[Diamond]])|16x [[Ender Pearl]] 48x [[Block of Redstone]] [[Block of Lapis Lazuli]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Block of Lapis Lazuli|Recipe: [[Block of Redstone]] x16 ×3 + Block of Lapis Lazuli x16 ×5 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|[[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]]|Lapis Lazuli IV (4) ([[Lapis Lazuli]])|[[Block of Lapis Lazuli]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Block of Lapis Lazuli|Recipe: Block of Lapis Lazuli x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]] x9|
|[[Weather Node]]|Prismarine Shard VI (6) ([[Prismarine Shard]])|[[Block of Lapis Lazuli]] 1x Block of Lapis Lazuli 8x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] ×8 + Block of Lapis Lazuli → [[Weather Node]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Block of Lapis Lazuli.|

# Block of Quartz
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Block of Quartz is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Block of Quartz.
[Block of Quartz]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]

Block of Quartz is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block that can be purchased from the [[Builder]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Block of Quartz can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 50 [[Coins]]. Up to 640 can be purchased per day.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|4x [[Nether Quartz]]|Recipe: [[Nether Quartz]] ×4 → Block of Quartz|
## Usage
### Private Island
A Block of Quartz can be placed as a decorate block on the player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]].
### Pure Quartz
Breakable Blocks of Quartz on [[Mining Islands]] represents [[Ores|Pure Quartz]], which has a [[Block Strength]] of 600 and can be broken to obtain [[Nether Quartz]].
### Crafting

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Nether Quartz]]|Nether Quartz IV (4) ([[Nether Quartz]])|[[Block of Quartz]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Block of Quartz|Recipe: Block of Quartz x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] x4|
|[[Quartz Stairs]]|None|[[Block of Quartz]] 6x Block of Quartz|Recipe: Block of Quartz ×6 → [[Quartz Stairs]] x4|
|[[Quartz Slab]]|None|[[Block of Quartz]] 3x Block of Quartz|Recipe: Block of Quartz ×3 → [[Quartz Slab]] x6|
|[[Pillar Quartz Block]]|None|[[Block of Quartz]] 2x Block of Quartz|Recipe: Block of Quartz ×2 → [[Pillar Quartz Block]]|

# Bogged
Categories: Vanilla objects, Sea Creatures, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs
Bogged is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Bogged.
You've hooked a Bogged!
[Bogged]
Type: Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]])
Mob Level: 25
Mob Type: [[Aquatic]] [[Skeletal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 3,000; [[Damage]]: 100
[Fishing Info]
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]): 5,000
Fishing Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Rod Type: Water ([[Sea Creatures]])
Fishing Level Requirement: Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])
[Drops]
Drops: 8–16x [[Mangrove Log]] 1–2x [[Sea Lumies]] 0–1x [[Aquamarine Dye]]
XP: +187 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 28
[Metadata]
ID: BOGGED_10
Bogged is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Spawning
Bogged can be fished up from water on [[Moonglade Marsh]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing level V (5)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Bogged is 5,000, resulting in a default catch chance of 50% (1 in 2 sea creatures) with all [[Sea Creatures]] unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase its weight:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Common Hook]]|1.25x|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You've hooked a Bogged!|
## Behavior
Bogged walk towards the player and fire arrows to deal damage. If the player gets too close, the Bogged throws its bow at the player as the phrase "Woops!" appears above its head and continues to hit the player with an arrow in its hand.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv25]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sea Lumies]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Sea Lumies]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Mangrove Log]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Mangrove Log]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Aquamarine Dye]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1/5M|
[Non-item drops - [Lv25]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|18|18|18|
|Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|187|187|187|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|28|28|28|
Bogged can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Bogged Shard]] and +75 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+750 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.
Item drops - [Lv25]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sea Lumies]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|30%|
|[[Mangrove Log]]|8|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|30%|

### Minions
Bones can also be obtained from [[Skeleton Minion|Skeleton Minions]].
### Shop Purchase
Bones can be purchased from the [[Combat Merchant]] for 8 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Bone]]|Bone V (5)|[[Bone]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Bone|Recipe: Bone x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Bone]]|
|Skeleton Minion I ([[Skeleton Minion]])|Bone I (1)|1x [[Wooden Sword]] [[Bone]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Bone|Recipe: Bone x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Sword]] → [[Skeleton Minion]]|
|Skeleton Minion II ([[Skeleton Minion]])|Bone I (1)|1x Skeleton Minion I ([[Skeleton Minion]]) [[Bone]] 160x Bone|Recipe: Bone x20 ×8 + [[Skeleton Minion]] → [[Skeleton Minion]]|
|Skeleton Minion III ([[Skeleton Minion]])|Bone I (1)|1x Skeleton Minion II ([[Skeleton Minion]]) [[Bone]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Bone|Recipe: Bone x40 ×8 + [[Skeleton Minion]] → [[Skeleton Minion]]|
|Skeleton Minion IV ([[Skeleton Minion]])|Bone I (1)|1x Skeleton Minion III ([[Skeleton Minion]]) [[Bone]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Bone|Recipe: Bone x64 ×8 + [[Skeleton Minion]] → [[Skeleton Minion]]|
|[[Bone Meal]]|None|[[Bone]] 1x Bone|Recipe: Bone → [[Bone Meal]] x3|
### Pet Upgrades
#### [[Skeleton Pet]]
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|[[Bone]] 512x Bone|5 Minutes|
## Tips
- Farming in the [[Spider's Den]] is one of the fastest ways to get Bones. This is because the [[Flint Skeleton|Flint Skeletons]] located there will always drop a bone, regardless of [[Auto-pickup]] or [[Looting]].
- After the Bone has dropped, wait a couple of seconds and the bone will spawn an additional [[Flint Skeleton]].
- Repeat this process to obtain significantly more bones compared to normal Skeletons.
- A good weapon is recommended that can one-shot [[Skeleton|Skeletons]]. It is recommended to have [[Auto-pickup]] unlocked.
- It is much more profitable to sell bones on the [[Bazaar]] instead of to [[Shops|Merchants]]
## Trivia
- When using a bow a bug sometimes occurs that causes collected bones to not count towards the collection.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Bone.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added [[Enchanted Bone Block]] to the collection.|

# Bone Meal
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Bone Meal is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Bone Meal.
[Bone Meal]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: INK_SACK:15
Tooltip: COMMON
Bone Meal is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that can be used on the player's [[Private Island]] or to dye items.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Bone]]|Recipe: [[Bone]] → Bone Meal x3|
### Starting Chest
3 Bone Meal can be found in the [[Chest]] that spawns on a new [[Private Island]].
## Usage
### Growing Crops
Bone Meal can be used on [[Grass]] or crops on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
### Spray Can
Bone Meal can be used in various [[Sprays]] in the [[Spray Can]].

|[[Gray Dye]]|None|[[Bone Meal]] 1x Bone Meal 1x [[Ink Sac]]|Recipe: [[Ink Sac]] + Bone Meal → [[Gray Dye]] x2|

# Bread

Bread is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Bread.
[Bread]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]

Bread is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that can be given to the [[Hungry Hiker]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|3x [[Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Wheat]] ×3 → Bread|
### Sam's Quest
During [[Sam]]'s Crop 'n Profit quest in [[The Garden]], she will ask the player for 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Wheat]], and give back 5 Bread in return.
## Usage
### Hungry Hiker
The [[Hungry Hiker]] requests food 10 different times during his quest. During this, he has a chance to ask for [[Bread]] 25 Bread.
### Jerry
During [[Sam]]'s Crop 'n Profit quest in [[The Garden]], [[Jerry]] will visit as a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]], and ask for 5 Bread. This only happens on his first offer during the quest.
### Great Spook
1 [[Great Spook Ring]], [[Bread]] 2 Bread, 1 [[Cooked Beef|Steak]], 1 [[Decorations|Onion]], 1 [[Decorations|Lettuce]], 1 [[Tasty Cheese]] and 1 [[French Fries]] can be used to buy a [[Great Spook Artifact]] from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].

# Brewing Stand
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Blocks, Pages with UIs, Craftable items, Brewing
Brewing Stand is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Brewing Stand.
[Brewing Stand]
Type: Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Alchemist]]
Buy: 30 [[Coins]]
Sell: 12 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BREWING_STAND_ITEM
Tooltip: COMMON
The Brewing Stand is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that is a core part of [[Potions|Brewing]], as nothing can be brewed without one. The easiest way to obtain one is to buy one from the [[Alchemist]] for 30 [[Coins]], or from crafting.
The Brewing Stand functions similarly to vanilla Minecraft and up to three [[Water Bottle|Water Bottles]]/[[Awkward Potion|Awkward Potions]]/[[Potions]] can be placed in the bottom three slots and a single brewing ingredient/modifier can be placed in the top slot. It takes 20 seconds for any brewing action to complete. See [[Potions|Brewing]] for more information.
Note: If you are having issues placing Water Bottles in a brewing stand, they are probably [[Glitched Items|Tagless Items]]. Throw them on the ground and pick them up to fix the issue.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Blaze Rod]] 3x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Blaze Rod]] + [[Cobblestone]] ×3 → Brewing Stand|
## Trivia
- While [[Hopper|Hoppers]] do work on Brewing Stands like in vanilla Minecraft, only vanilla [[Potions]] can be brewed.
- A [[Witch Pet]] can reduce the time for brewing by up to half.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Brewing Stand.|
|Oct 17, 2021|The Brewing Stand is temporarily disabled.|
|Nov 2, 2021|The Brewing Stand is no longer disabled.|
|Sep 6, 2022|The Brewing Stand is temporarily disabled.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Splash Potions can no longer be modified through Brewing Stands.|
Linked from: [[Absorption Potion]], [[Adrenaline Potion]], [[Agility Potion]], [[Alchemy]], [[Archery Potion]], [[Bitter Iced Tea]], [[Black Coffee]], [[Blaze Rod]], [[Blindness Potion]], [[Burning Potion]], [[Changelog/2020/July 21]], [[Cheap Coffee]], [[Cobblestone]], [[Cold Resistance Potion]], [[Critical Potion]], [[Damage Potion]], [[Dctr. Paper]], [[Decent Coffee]], [[Dodge Potion]], [[Enchanted Rabbit Foot]], [[Experience Potion]], [[Fire Resistance Potion]], [[Furniture]], [[Furniture/Carpentry]], [[God Potion]], [[God Potion (Legacy)]], [[God Potion Effect]], [[Haste Potion]], [[Healing Potion]], [[Invisibility Potion]], [[Jump Boost Potion]], [[KnockOff™ Cola]], [[Knockback Potion]], [[Mana Potion]], [[Mixins]], [[Night Vision Potion]], [[Pet Luck Potion]], [[Planks]], [[Poison Potion]], [[Potions]] (+22 more)

Bucket is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Bucket.
[Bucket]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 4 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BUCKET
Tooltip: COMMON
A Bucket is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that can be turned into a [[Water Bucket]], [[Lava Bucket]], or [[Milk Bucket]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|3x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×3 → Bucket|
### Consuming Buckets
Consuming a [[Water Bucket]], [[Lava Bucket]], or [[Milk Bucket]] will turn it back into a Bucket.
## Usage
### Filling Up
A Bucket can be converted into a [[Water Bucket]] by right clicking it on a water source, into a [[Lava Bucket]] by right clicking it on a [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] block, or into a [[Milk Bucket]] by right clicking it on a [[Cow]] or [[Mushroom Cow|Mooshroom]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Bucket.|

## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|100|[[Cactus Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|250|[[Cactus Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|III|500|[[Resistance Potion]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Enchanted Cactus Green]] Recipe|+4|
|V|2,500|[[Desert Island]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Piercing]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Thorns]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|VIII|25,000|[[Enchanted Cactus]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|50,000|+50,000 [[Farming]]Experience|+4|
## Obtaining
Cactus can be harvested at the [[Mushroom Desert]] or [[The Garden]], generated by [[Cactus Minion|Cactus Minions]], and bought from the [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]] for 15 [[Coins]] each.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Portal to Mushroom Island ([[Portals]])|Cocoa Beans III (3) ([[Cocoa Beans]])|16x [[Ender Pearl]] 48x [[Cocoa Beans]] [[Cactus]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Cactus|Recipe: [[Cocoa Beans]] x16 ×3 + Cactus x16 ×5 + [[Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Portals]]|
|Cactus Minion I ([[Cactus Minion]])|Cactus I (1)|1x [[Wooden Hoe]] [[Cactus]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Cactus|Recipe: Cactus x16 ×8 + [[Wooden Hoe]] → [[Cactus Minion]]|
|Cactus Minion II ([[Cactus Minion]])|Cactus I (1)|1x Cactus Minion I ([[Cactus Minion]]) [[Cactus]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Cactus|Recipe: Cactus x32 ×8 + [[Cactus Minion]] → [[Cactus Minion]]|
|Cactus Minion III ([[Cactus Minion]])|Cactus I (1)|1x Cactus Minion II ([[Cactus Minion]]) [[Cactus]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Cactus|Recipe: Cactus x64 ×8 + [[Cactus Minion]] → [[Cactus Minion]]|
|Cactus Helmet ([[Cactus Armor]])|Cactus II (2)|[[Cactus]] 5x Cactus|Recipe: Cactus ×5 → [[Cactus Armor]]|
|Cactus Chestplate ([[Cactus Armor]])|Cactus II (2)|[[Cactus]] 8x Cactus|Recipe: Cactus ×8 → [[Cactus Armor]]|
|Cactus Leggings ([[Cactus Armor]])|Cactus II (2)|[[Cactus]] 7x Cactus|Recipe: Cactus ×7 → [[Cactus Armor]]|
|Cactus Boots ([[Cactus Armor]])|Cactus II (2)|[[Cactus]] 4x Cactus|Recipe: Cactus ×4 → [[Cactus Armor]]|
|[[Desert Island]]|Cactus V (5)|5x [[Sugar Cane]] [[Cactus]] 10x Cactus 30x [[Sand]]|Recipe: Cactus x5 ×2 + [[Sugar Cane]] x5 + [[Sand]] x5 ×6 → [[Desert Island]]|
|[[Enchanted Cactus Green]]|Cactus IV (4)|[[Cactus]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Cactus|Recipe: Cactus x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Cactus Green]]|
### Brewing
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Resistance I Potion ([[Resistance Potion]])|Cactus III (3)|Cactus +; [[Awkward Potion]]|Recipe: Cactus → [[Resistance Potion]], [[Resistance Potion]], [[Resistance Potion]]|
### Smelting
|Name|Ingredients|Recipe|
|-|-|-|
|[[Cactus Green]]|Cactus|Recipe: Cactus + [[Coal]] → [[Cactus Green]]|

# Cake
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items
Cake is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Cake.
[Cake]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 10 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CAKE
Tooltip: COMMON
A Cake is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block that can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Egg]] 2x [[Sugar]] 3x [[Milk Bucket]] 3x [[Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Milk Bucket]] ×3 + [[Sugar]] ×2 + [[Egg]] + [[Wheat]] ×3 → Cake|
## Usage
### Private Island
A Cake can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. Right-clicking it will not consume any slices.
## Trivia
- During the December 2023 [[Bingo|Secret Bingo]], the "Party Of One" goal required the player to place a cake and eat it.

[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Cake.|

|[[Golden Carrot]]|None|[[Carrot]] 1x Carrot 8x [[Gold Nugget]]|Recipe: [[Gold Nugget]] ×8 + Carrot → [[Golden Carrot]]|
|[[Simple Carrot Candy]]|Carrot II (2)|[[Carrot]] 576x (9 stacks (576 items total)) Carrot|Recipe: Carrot x64 ×9 → [[Simple Carrot Candy]]|
|[[Rabbit Stew]]|None|[[Carrot]] 1x Carrot 1x [[Bowl]] 1x [[Cooked Rabbit]] 1x [[Baked Potato]] 1x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Cooked Rabbit]] + Carrot + [[Baked Potato]] + [[Mushroom]] + [[Bowl]] → [[Rabbit Stew]]|
|[[Rabbit Stew]]|None|1x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x [[Bowl]] 1x [[Cooked Rabbit]] 1x [[Baked Potato]] [[Carrot]] 1x Carrot|Recipe: [[Cooked Rabbit]] + Carrot + [[Baked Potato]] + [[Mushroom]] + [[Bowl]] → [[Rabbit Stew]]|
## Gallery
- Carrot in the Greenhouse Carrot in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Carrots
Carrots is a glitched item dropped from [[Frosty (mob)|Frosty]] (the mob) when it was added in 0.7.5. Unlike most of the other glitched items, Carrots can be put on the [[Auction House]]. For some reason, the game treats Carrots as a block, and it can only be found in the "Blocks" section of the [[Auction House]].
## Trivia
- It used to be possible to obtain a carrot on a stick named "carrot" when crafting in the 2x2 vanilla crafting table, however this has been patched.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Carrot.|
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Increased the NPC sell price of Carrot from 1 Coin ([[Coins]]) to 4 [[Coins]].|
|May 31, 2023|0.18.5|Reduced the NPC sell price of Carrot from 4 [[Coins]] to 3 [[Coins]].|

# Charcoal
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Minion Modifiers
Charcoal is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Charcoal.
[Charcoal]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHARCOAL
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Increases the speed of your minion by 5% for 30; minutes!; COMMON
Charcoal is an [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Minion Fuel]] that can increases the speed of [[Minions]] by 5% for 30 minutes.
## Obtaining
### Smelting
|Ingredients|Recipe|
|-|-|
|1x Log ([[Wood (disambiguation)]])|Recipe: [[Oak Log]]/[[Birch Log]]/[[Spruce Log]]/[[Dark Oak Log]]/[[Acacia Log]]/[[Jungle Log]] + [[Coal]] → Charcoal|
### Harvesting
Charcoal can be obtained by harvesting [[Oak Log|Oak Logs]], [[Birch Log|Birch Logs]], [[Spruce Log|Spruce Logs]], [[Dark Oak Log|Dark Oak Logs]], [[Acacia Log|Acacia Logs]], [[Jungle Log|Jungle Logs]] with the [[Smelting Touch]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] applied to the used tool.
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Charcoal can be used as a fuel source for [[Minions]]. It increases the efficiency by 5% for 30 minutes. Charcoal can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Torch]]|None|[[Charcoal]] 1x Charcoal 1x [[Stick]]|Recipe: Charcoal + [[Stick]] → [[Torch]] x4|
|[[Block of Coal]]|None|[[Charcoal]] 9x Charcoal|Recipe: Charcoal ×9 → [[Block of Coal]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Charcoal.|
|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|Charcoal can now be obtained from the [[Smelting Touch]] Enchantment ([[Enchantments]]).|
|Jul 8, 2019|Charcoal can now be used as [[Minion Fuel]].|
|Aug 13, 2021|Charcoal can now be sold to NPCs ([[NPC]]).|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Fixed Furnaces ([[Furnace]]) returning a Charcoal without the [[Minion Fuel]] properties.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed not being able to craft a [[Block of Coal]] with Charcoal.|

# Charged Creeper
Categories: Vanilla objects, Historical content, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, The Uprising
Charged Creeper is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Charged Creeper.
Charged Creeper was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
[Charged Creeper]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 50
Spawn Condition: [[Ancient Pig]] (Thunder ([[Thunder (event)]]) event)

[Metadata]
ID: SHRINE_CHARGED_CREEPER_50
The Charged Creeper was a mob that spawned during the [[Ancient Pig]]'s [[Thunder (event)|Thunder]] event.
## Spawning
Charged Creeper could spawn on any [[Locations|Public Island]] near other [[Mob|Mobs]] during [[Ancient Pig]]'s [[Thunder (event)|Thunder]] event.
## Behavior
Standing near a Charged Creeper caused it to blow up, dealing 300 [[Damage]] and knocking the player by 1 block.

# Chicken
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, More info needed, Mobs with Bestiary data
Chicken is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Chicken.

Chickens are passive [[Mob|Mobs]] found in [[The Barn]].
## Behavior
Chickens are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player.
Every so often, they will lay an [[Egg]] on the ground[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.). They can also be brought to [[Jake]] in the [[Desert Mountain]] using a [[Lead]] to unlock the [[Advanced Chicken Axe]] in his shop.

|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|

Chickens are passive [[Mob|Mobs]] found on the [[Private Island]] and from [[Chicken Minion|Chicken Minions]].
## Spawning
Chickens spawn naturally on [[Grass]] on the [[Private Island]], and from [[Chicken Minion|Chicken Minions]].
### Stranded
This content is exclusive to the [[Stranded]] gamemode.
On [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], Chickens that spawn in [[Biome Stick|Forest Biomes]] will have triple health and double drops.
## Behavior
Chickens are passive mobs that slowly roam around the area they're in. They do not attack the player, but will run away if attacked by the player.
Every so often, they will lay an [[Egg]] on the ground[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.). Chickens spawned by a [[Chicken Minion]] with an [[Enchanted Egg]] will always lay an [[Egg]] directly after spawning.

## Collection

|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|VI|5,000|[[Travel Scroll to the Gold Mine]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Enchanted Coal Block]] Recipe; Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|VIII|25,000|[[Enchanted Lava Bucket]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|50,000|Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]) Recipe|+4|

## Obtaining
### Mining
[[Ores|Coal Ore]] and [[Ores|Pure Coal]] can be mined to obtain Coal.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv477]|[[Bladesoul]]|20|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv477]|[[Bladesoul]]|10|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|70%|
|[Lv477]|[[Bladesoul]]|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|

### Minions
Coal can be obtained from [[Coal Minion|Coal Minions]], or from [[Wood (disambiguation)|Wood Minions]] with the [[Auto Smelter]] upgrade applied.
### Trading
|Requirement|Trade Input|Trade Output|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Oak Log]] →|→ Coal x2|

|-|-|-|

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Auto Smelter]]|Cobblestone III (3) ([[Cobblestone]])|[[Coal]] 1x Coal 64x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] x8 ×8 + Coal → [[Auto Smelter]]|
|Coal Minion I ([[Coal Minion]])|Coal I (1)|1x [[Wooden Pickaxe]] [[Coal]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Coal|Recipe: Coal x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Pickaxe]] → [[Coal Minion]]|
|Coal Minion II ([[Coal Minion]])|Coal I (1)|1x Coal Minion I ([[Coal Minion]]) [[Coal]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Coal|Recipe: Coal x20 ×8 + [[Coal Minion]] → [[Coal Minion]]|
|Coal Minion III ([[Coal Minion]])|Coal I (1)|1x Coal Minion II ([[Coal Minion]]) [[Coal]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Coal|Recipe: Coal x40 ×8 + [[Coal Minion]] → [[Coal Minion]]|
|Coal Minion IV ([[Coal Minion]])|Coal I (1)|1x Coal Minion III ([[Coal Minion]]) [[Coal]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Coal|Recipe: Coal x64 ×8 + [[Coal Minion]] → [[Coal Minion]]|
|[[Enchanted Charcoal]]|Coal V (5)|32 Log ([[Wood (disambiguation)]]) (Vanilla) [[Coal]] 128 (2 stacks) Coal|Recipe: Coal x32 ×6 + [[Oak Log]] x32/[[Birch Log]] x32/[[Spruce Log]] x32/[[Dark Oak Log]] x32/[[Acacia Log]] x32/[[Jungle Log]] x32 → [[Enchanted Charcoal]]|
|[[Hard Glass]]|Sand VII (7) ([[Sand]])|1x [[Enchanted Sand]] [[Coal]] 8x Coal|Recipe: Coal ×8 + [[Enchanted Sand]] → [[Hard Glass]] x8|
|[[Torch]]|None|[[Coal]] 1x Coal 1x [[Stick]]|Recipe: Coal + [[Stick]] → [[Torch]] x4|
|Beginner Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])|Coal II (2)|1x [[Leather]] [[Coal]] 8x Coal|Recipe: Coal ×8 + [[Leather]] → [[Mining Sack]]|
|[[Block of Coal]]|None|[[Coal]] 9x Coal|Recipe: Coal ×9 → [[Block of Coal]]|
### Furnace Fuel
Coal can be used as fuel in [[Furnace|Furnaces]].
### Minion Fuel
Coal increases minion speed by 5% for 30 minutes. Coal can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Brewing
Coal can be used to brew [[Haste Potion|Haste Potions]].
## Trivia
- The item description is frequently changed, rendering some Coal not stackable. This can be fixed by dropping old Coal on the floor or switching lobbies to update all the items to the latest description.

- An example of a pure Coal vein

# Commands
Aliases: /ah, /craftedgenerators, /deathcount, /enderchest, /hub, /invite, /map, /myfilter, /pickupstash, /pity, /profiles, /sbmen, /scg, /sethome, /setspawn, /togglechat, /togglemusic, /togglerds, /viewcollection, /viewcraftingtable, /viewpetsmenu, /viewpotion, /viewquestlog, /viewrecipe, /viewsettings, /viewskill, /visit, /warp, /whereami, /wtfmap, /zoo, Command, Playtime, Useful Commands, Warping
Categories: Vanilla objects, Confirmations needed, Mechanics
Commands is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Commands.
Commands are features activated by typing certain strings of text into the chatbox. Commands allow the server and the player to do certain things that are not accessible in vanilla Minecraft. In a Minecraft world, use T+/ or simply / to enter the command mode.
## Explanation
|Term|Meaning|
|-|-|
|Command Name|A command name is the exact string typed on keyboard to invoke a command function.|
|Argument Name|Some commands accept one or more values called Arguments placed after the command name. Each argument is separated by a space. An argument name is name that describes what value is expected in a place.|
|Argument Specifications|Each argument has an expected type or expected values. For example, an argument called "width" may expect an integer that represents a width in its place. An argument called "mode" may expect one of the two exact values team and solo. The argument specification is provided for each argument on this page.|
QUICK TIP
An argument name only describes what an argument means, not the exact value to put in place. The requirement on the exact value is described in the argument specifications.
Syntax used to describe a command throughout this page:
|Symbol|Name|Description|Example|
|-|-|-|-|
|< >|Angle Brackets|An argument field ("Field"). Describes what arguments to put in a position using argument names.|<player>|
|/|Single Bar|One of the arguments should be chosen for this field.|<width/height>|
|?|Question Mark|The preceding field is optional.|<width>?|
|*|Asterisk|There can be zero to many of the preceding field.|<player>*|
|+|Plus|There can be one to many of the preceding field.|<player>+|
## SkyBlock-specific Commands
The commands listed below are SkyBlock specific. Any requirement for a player to be on SkyBlock to use a command is omitted below.
### Basic Commands
Commands that are not categorized are listed below.
- /claimdarewards
- Claims any unclaimed [[Dark Auction]] rewards.
Argument Specifications:
- /evacuate
- Leave current server. Condition: Only when the server is restarting soon
Argument Specifications:
- /deathcount
- Shows total death count of all members on current profile.
Argument Specifications:
- /playtime; /pt
- Shows total playtime on current profile.
Argument Specifications:
- /togglemusic
- Turns music on/off for certain areas.
Argument Specifications:
- /togglerds
- Toggles the sound that plays when receiving a rare drop.
Argument Specifications:
### Social Commands
Commands related to [[Social]] and [[Guesting]] are listed below.
- /acceptinvite
- Accepts an island guesting invite from someone.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
Argument Specifications:
- /invite <player>
- Send a player an invite to Guest ([[Guesting]]) own private island (works also when island visiting is disabled).
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
- /islandname <island_name>
- Changes the Island Name ([[Social]]) of own island. Condition: Requires Social XX (20) ([[Social]])
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|island_name|required|string|A new name to set as island name|
- /islandrank <player> <rank_name>
- Changes the Island Rank ([[Social]]) of a player on own island. Condition: Requires Social IX (9) ([[Social]])
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
|rank_name|required|string|An island rank defined in island settings|
- /sbkick <player>
- Kicks a Guest from own island. Condition: Only when on own [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]]
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
- /sbkickall
- Kicks all Guests from own island. Condition: Only when on own [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]]
Argument Specifications:
- /setguestlocation; /setguestspawn; /guestlocation
- Sets the spawn for guests on own private island to the block you are currently standing on.
Argument Specifications:
- /setspawnlocation; /spawnlocation; /setspawn; /sethome
- Sets the spawn on own private island to the block currently standing on.
Argument Specifications:
- /social <game_id>?
- Displays available games, or plays a game on own island. Condition: Only when on own [[Private Island]]; Requires Social IV (4) ([[Social]])
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|game_id|optional|string|A case-insensitive game ID representing the game to play. One of: scrambled quickmaths pingwars|
- /visit <player>; /guest <player>
- Lets you Guest ([[Guesting]]) on someone's island (if they have visiting turned on). You can't /visit people who are in a co-op with you.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
### Dungeon Commands
Commands related to [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] are listed below.
- /joininstance <instanceType>; /joindungeon <instanceType>; /joinkuudra <instanceType>
- Joins a Dungeon ([[Dungeons]]) or [[Kuudra]] game.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|instanceType|required|string|A case insensitive dungeon name with spaces replaced with underscores. e.g. CATACOMBS_FLOOR_ONE MASTER_CATACOMBS_FLOOR_SIX KUUDRA_INFERNAL|
- /showextrastats
- Displays extra statistics for a Dungeon run in game message. Condition: Only right after a Dungeon run is completed
Argument Specifications:
- /viewdungeonclass <class_id>
- Opens the perks for a Dungeon Class ([[Classes]]).
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|class_id|required|string|A lowercase class name with spaces replaced with underscores. One of: tank berserk mage healer archer|
- /viewdungeontype <dungeon_id>
- Opens the reward tree of a Dungeon ([[Dungeons]]).
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|dungeon_id|required|string|A case insensitive dungeon name with spaces replaced with underscores. One of: catacombs|
### Garden Commands
Commands related to [[The Garden|Garden]] are listed below.
- /barnskinsmenu; /barnskins
- Opens the [[Barn Skins]] menu. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]
Argument Specifications:
- /cropmilestonesmenu; /cropmilestones
- Opens the Crop Milestones ([[The Garden]]) menu. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]
Argument Specifications:
- /cropupgradesmenu; /cropupgrades
- Opens the Crop Updates ([[The Garden]]) menu. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]
Argument Specifications:
- /deskmenu; /desk
- Opens The Desk ([[The Garden]]) menu. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]
Argument Specifications:
- /gardenlevelsmenu; /gardenlevels
- Opens the Garden Levels ([[The Garden]]) menu. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]
Argument Specifications:
- /plotteleport <plotName>; /tptoplot <plotName>; /plottp <plotName>
- Teleport to a Plot. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]. Only when destination is unlocked.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|plotName|required|string|The name of the destination plot.|
- /visitormilestonesmenu; /visitormilestones
- Opens the Visitor Milestones ([[The Garden]]) menu. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]
Argument Specifications:
- /setyaw [<yaw>]
- Sets the stored yaw on the held [[Squeaky Mousemat]]. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]. Must be holding a [[Squeaky Mousemat]].
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|yaw|optional|float|The yaw to set.|
- /setpitch [<pitch>]
- Sets the stored pitch on the held [[Squeaky Mousemat]]. Condition: Only when in [[The Garden]]. Must be holding a [[Squeaky Mousemat]].
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|pitch|optional|float|The pitch to set.|
### Tool Use Commands
Commands regarding tool use and the [[Stash|Item Stash]] are listed below.
- /clearstash
- Removes all items currently in the Item Stash ([[Stash]]).
Argument Specifications:
- /pickupstash
- Takes all items currently in the Item Stash ([[Stash]]).
Argument Specifications:
- /undograndarchitect
- Undo action from [[Builder's Wand]]. Condition: Only when recently used the zapping ability
Argument Specifications:
- /undozap
- Undo action from [[Block Zapper]]. Condition: Only when recently used the zapping ability
Argument Specifications:
- /viewstash
- Shows all items currently in the Item Stash ([[Stash]]) in chat.
Argument Specifications:
- /setmaxspeed [<speed>]
- Sets the speed cap of [[Rancher's Boots]]. Condition: Must be wearing [[Rancher's Boots]].
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|speed|optional|int|The speed cap to set.|
### Teleportation Commands
Commands that sends the player to various locations are listed below.
- /enterthecrystalhollows
- Teleports player to [[Crystal Hollows]]. Condition: Only after summoning a minecart using [[Gwendolyn]]
Argument Specifications:
- /hub
- Teleports player to the [[Hub Island]]. In SkyBlock, unlike other environments, /hub is not synonymous with /lobby. Same as: /warp hub
Argument Specifications:
- /garry
- Teleports player to [[Old Man Garry]] Condition: Only when a Dwarven Mines Event ([[Dwarven Mines]]) is about to start
Argument Specifications:
- /tptodonexpresso
- Teleports player to Don Expresso Condition: Only when a Mithril Gourmond Event ([[Dwarven Mines]]) is active
Argument Specifications:
- /is; /island; /privateisland
- Sends player to own [[Private Island]]. Same as: /warp home
Argument Specifications:
- /savethejerrys
- Teleports player to [[Jerry's Workshop]]. Condition: Only during [[Season of Jerry]]
Argument Specifications:
- /warp <location_id>?; /warps <location_id>?
- Opens the [[Fast Travel]] menu, or warps the player to the specified location. Condition: Corresponding [[Travel Scrolls]] shall be consumed for a location warp to unlock (except: hub/home)
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|location_id|optional|string|A case-insensitive location name with spaces replaced by underscores. Location IDs can be found in the [[Fast Travel]] menu.|
- /warpforge
- Teleports player to [[The Forge]].
Argument Specifications:
### Interface Commands (Guides)
Commands that opens a game UI for guides are listed below.
- /enchantmentsguide; /enchantments; /eg
- Opens the Enchantments Guide ([[Enchantments]]) menu.
Argument Specifications:
- /seacreatureguide; /fishingbosses; /seacreature; /scg
- Opens the Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) Guide menu.
Argument Specifications:
### Interface Commands (Lookup)
Commands that opens a game UI for specific lookups are listed below.
QUICK TIP
An item's ID can be found on the page for that item, under "Item Metadata" in the infobox.
- /viewauction <auction_id>
- Shows the item for Auction ([[Auction House]]) and the actions available. Condition: If without Cookie Buff ([[Booster Cookie]]), usage limited on [[Hub Island]]
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|auction_id|required|string|A string of hexidecimal digits|
- /viewbosscollection <boss_id>
- Opens the boss collection for that specific boss.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|boss_id|required|string|A lowercase boss name with spaces replaced with underscores. One of: catacombs_1 (Bonzo) catacombs_2 (Scarf) catacombs_3 (The Professor) catacombs_4 (Thorn) catacombs_5 (Livid) catacombs_6 (Sadan) catacombs_7 (Necron) kuudra|
- /viewcollection <item_id>
- Shows the Collection ([[Collections]]) menu for a collection item.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|item_id|required|string|An all-uppercase item ID of a collection item, usually the item name with spaces replaced with underscores. Ex: SUGAR_CANE is a valid item ID.|
- /viewpotion <potion_id>
- Opens the Potion ([[Potions]]) Recipe menu for a potion.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|potion_id|required|string|A lowercase potion name with spaces replaced with underscores. Ex: combat is a valid potion name.|
- /viewrecipe <item_id>
- Shows the Recipe ([[Crafting]]) menu of an item (works also when item is not unlocked).
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|item_id|required|string|An all-uppercase item ID, usually the item name with spaces replaced with underscores. Ex: DAY_SAVER is a valid item ID.|
- /viewskill <skill_id>
- Opens the Skill ([[Skills]]) menu for a skill.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|skill_id|required|string|A case-insensitive skill name with spaces replaced with underscores. Ex: combat is a valid skill name.|

### Cookie Buff Commands

Argument Specifications:

Argument Specifications:

- /bazaar <search_prompt>?; /baz <search_prompt>?; /bz <search_prompt>?

- Opens the [[Potion Bag]] menu. Condition: Only with Cookie Buff ([[Booster Cookie]]) and Nether Wart II (2) ([[Nether Wart]])
Argument Specifications:
{{CommandPageRow|command=/thehex /hecks /hex|desc=Opens the [[The Hex|Hex]] menu. |cond=Only with [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] and Prosperous [[Museum]] Rank
- /pity
- Opens the Pity ([[Pity and RNG Meters]]) menu. Condition: Only with Cookie Buff ([[Booster Cookie]])

### Co-op Commands
Commands related to [[Co-op]] are listed below.
- /coop <player>+
- Creates a co-op profile with other players.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|one or more|string|A player's in-game name|
- /coopadd <player>
- Adds a player to the current co-op profile. Only add trusted people to a co-op profile.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
- /coopchat <message>; /cchat <message>; /cc <message>
- Sends one message in the co-op channel.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|message|required|string|A message to send to the co-op channel.|
- /coopkick <player>
- Removes a player from own co-op.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
- /coopmanage
- Manage the current co-op profile.
Argument Specifications:
- /coopcheck
- Shows outgoing co-op invites and actions available.
Argument Specifications:
- /coopid
- Shows ID of the current co-op.
Argument Specifications:
- /coopmode <gamemode> <player>+
- Creates a co-op profile of a Gamemode ([[Gamemodes]]) with other players.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|gamemode|required|string|A lowercase or case-insensitive (unconfirmed) Gamemode name. One of: normal ironman stranded|
|player|one or more|string|A player's in-game name|
- /coopparty
- Creates a party and sends all members of a co-op a party invite.
Argument Specifications:
- /coopsalvage <player>
- Salvages items for members who left the co-op. Condition: Only when on a co-op profile. Only works on a player who left the co-op.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
- /coopview <player>
- View a co-op invitation sent from a player.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
- /togglecoopchat; /co
- Enters co-op channel. Subsequent messages are sent to the co-op channel by default. Same as: /chat skyblock-coop; /chat coop
Argument Specifications:
### Miscellaneous Commands
Commands that don't fit in any other category are listed below.
- /purchasecrystallhollowspass
- Renews your Crystal Hollows pass for 10,000 coins. Used in a chat prompt. (Yes, this is spelled as "crystall".) Condition: Only when you're currently in the Crystal Hollows and your pass expires in less than a minute.
Argument Specifications:
- /killaura
- Sends you to a prison cell in hub for 5 minutes Condition: When Aura is mayor
Argument Specifications:
### Removed Commands
Commands that were removed are listed below.
- /killdante
- Instantly kills Slime [[Dante]]. It was likely enabled for all players by accident.
Argument Specifications:
- /opencannonbuymenu
- Opens the Buy Snow Cannon menu of [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]].
Argument Specifications:
- /slimetest <size>
- Summons a Slime ([[Slime (disambiguation)]]) of any size. It was likely enabled for all players by accident.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|size|required (unconfirmed)|int|A number that represents the size value of the slime. The actual size of the slime are 0.51 blocks times the size value in each dimension.|
- /viewachievementmenu <achievement_id>
- Opens tiered or challenge achievements ([[Achievements]]) of SkyBlock.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|achievement_id|required|int|A lowercase or case-insensitive (unconfirmed) string that is an achievement ID. Ex: skyblock_spiky and skyblock_combat are valid achievement IDs.|
- /wiki
- Shows site link towards the official Hypixel Wiki.
Argument Specifications:
- /wikiinhand; /wikihand; /wikithis
- Shows page link of the item held towards the official Hypixel Wiki. Condition: Only when holding an item
Argument Specifications:
## Non SkyBlock-specific Commands
The commands listed below can be used outside of SkyBlock on [[Hypixel]]. Only useful commands are listed below.
See a full list of Hypixel commands on the Hypixel Wiki on Commands.
### Useful Commands
- /friend; /f
- Lists the commands for the friend feature for player bonding features. Same as: /friend help; /f help
Argument Specifications:
- /guild; /g
- Lists the commands for the guild feature for gaining stats and rewards as a group. Same as: /guild help; /g help
Argument Specifications:
- /ignore
- Lists the commands for the player filter feature for ignoring interactions from other players.
Argument Specifications:
- /lobby; /l
- Sends a player to the last game lobby joined. Sends a player to Main Lobby if player is already in a game lobby.
Argument Specifications:
- /locraw
- Shows a JSON string representation of information on current server. Useful when reporting a bug.
Argument Specifications:
- /particlequality <quality_level>; /quality <quality_level>; /pq <quality_level>
- Changes the particle rendering quality. Useful when smoother rendering is needed.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|
|quality_level|string|A case-insensitive string of a valid quality level. One of: off low medium high extreme|
- /party; /p
- Lists the commands for the partying feature for group gameplay features. Same as: /party help; /p help
Argument Specifications:
- /recentservers; /wherewasi; /rs
- Shows server recently connected. Useful when reporting a bug.
Argument Specifications:
- /report <player>
- Report a player for breaking server rules.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
- /settings
- Opens the General Settings menu for the Hypixel server.
Argument Specifications:
- /skyblock
- Sends a player to [[SkyBlock]].
Argument Specifications:
- /whereami
- Shows the name of the current server. Useful when reporting a bug.
Argument Specifications:
- /wtfmap; /map
- Shows the name of the current map. Useful when reporting a bug.
Argument Specifications:
### Useful Chat Commands
- /chat <channel>
- Enters a channel. Subsequent messages are sent to the channel by default. Condition: Party channel requires being in a party.; Guild channel requires being in a guild.; Officer channel requires being in a guild and being an officer.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|channel|required|string|A case-insensitive channel name. One of: all/a party/p guild/g officer/o skyblock-coop/coop|
- /guild chat <message>; /gchat <message>; /gc <message>
- Sends one message in the guild channel. Condition: Only when in a guild
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|message|required|string|A message to send to the co-op channel.|
- /message <player> <message>; /whisper <player> <message>; /msg <player> <message>; /w <player> <message>
- Send a message privately to a player.
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|player|required|string|A player's in-game name|
|message|required|string|A message to send.|
- /myfilter
- Lists the commands for the chat filter feature for filtering user-defined words in chat.
Argument Specifications:
- /officer chat <message>; /ochat <message>; /oc <message>
- Sends one message in the guild channel. Condition: Only when in a guild and is an officer in guild
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|message|required|string|A message to send to the co-op channel.|
- /party chat <message>; /pchat <message>; /pc <message>
- Sends one message in the party channel. Condition: Only when in a party
Argument Specifications:
|Name|Field Type|Data Type|Value|
|-|-|-|-|
|message|required|string|A message to send to the co-op channel.|
- /reply <message>; /r <message>
- Send a message privately to the player most recently received a private message from. Condition: Only if a private message was received in the past 5 minutes
Argument Specifications:
- /togglechat
- Toggles chat visibility: Shows/hides messages from other players.
Argument Specifications:

# Comparator
Aliases: Redstone Comparator
Categories: Vanilla objects, Disabled Features, Common items, Items, Craftable items
Comparator is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Comparator.
This item or feature is currently disabled and cannot be used.
[Comparator]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 5 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REDSTONE_COMPARATOR
Tooltip: COMMON
A Comparator is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks|Redstone-related]] block that can be obtained but not used.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Nether Quartz]] 3x [[Stone]] 3x [[Redstone Torch]]|Recipe: [[Redstone Torch]] ×3 + [[Nether Quartz]] + [[Stone]] ×3 → Comparator|
### Shop Purchase
A Comparator can be purchased from the [[Mad Redstone Engineer]] for 10 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Private Island
Comparators cannot be placed down, and therefore cannot be used.
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|You cannot use this right now.|Trying to place a Comparator|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Comparator.|
|May 27, 2026|Disabled Comparators.|
Linked from: [[Nether Quartz]], [[Redstone Torch]], [[Settings]], [[Stone]], [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks]]

# Cooked Beef
Aliases: Steak
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items
Cooked Beef is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Cooked Beef.
[Cooked Beef]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 4 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: COOKED_BEEF
Tooltip: COMMON
Cooked Beef is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that can be given to the [[Hungry Hiker]].
## Obtaining
### Smelting
|Ingredients|Recipe|
|-|-|
|1x [[Raw Beef]]|Recipe: [[Raw Beef]] + [[Coal]] → Cooked Beef|
## Usage
### Hungry Hiker
The [[Hungry Hiker]] requests food 10 different times during his quest. During this, he has a chance to ask for [[Cooked Beef]] 30 Cooked Beef.
### Great Spook
1 [[Great Spook Ring]], 2 [[Bread]], [[Cooked Beef]] 1 Cooked Beef, 1 [[Decorations|Onion]], 1 [[Decorations|Lettuce]], 1 [[Tasty Cheese]] and 1 [[French Fries]] can be used to buy a [[Great Spook Artifact]] from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Cooked Beef.|
Linked from: [[Accessories/Obtained From NPC Shop]], [[Bread]], [[Burger (disambiguation)]], [[French Fries]], [[Great Spook Artifact]], [[Great Spook Ring]], [[Hungry Hiker]], [[Tasty Cheese]], [[Tyashoi Alchemist]], [[Vanilla Objects]]

# Coordinates
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mechanics
Coordinates is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Coordinates.
Coordinates numerically represent a [[Player]]'s location in a Minecraft world. They are based on a grid where three lines or axes intersect at the origin point. Players initially spawn within a couple hundred blocks of the origin point.
## Usage
- The x-axis indicates the player's distance east (positive) or west (negative) of the origin point—e.x., the longitude
- The z-axis indicates the player's distance south (positive) or north (negative) of the origin point—e.x., the latitude
- The y-axis indicates how high or low (from 0 to 255, with 64 being sea level) the player is—e.x., the elevation
To remember which axis is which, one may use the Right-hand rule - middle-finger points at x-axis, thumb points at y-axis, index-finger points at z-axis.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Coordinates.|

# Crafting
Aliases: Quick Crafting, Quick crafting, Recipe, Shapeless, Shapeless Recipes, Super Craft, Super craft, Supercraft, Supercrafting
Categories: Vanilla objects, Craftable items, Pages with UIs, Mechanics
Crafting is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Crafting.
Crafting Table
Crafting is a gameplay mechanic that allows the player to obtain new items with some items as ingredients. Crafting is awarded with [[Carpentry]] skill experience.
In SkyBlock, a Recipe, synonymous with Crafting Recipe, specifies the amounts and positions of ingredients for crafting.
The gameplay mechanic is an expansion of the vanilla Minecraft mechanic with the same name. In SkyBlock, crafting has a different menu and is more accessible in the sense that players don't need to craft a [[Crafting Table]] since it's also available in the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
SkyBlock also has various other recipes that are not available in vanilla Minecraft to make obtaining custom items possible.
## Usage
To craft, players can use the 2×2 crafting grid in their inventory (Press E by default), crafting the [[Crafting Table]] or using a pre-existing one in the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
It's worth noting that the 2×2 in the inventory doesn't support custom items that are not from vanilla Minecraft. Items crafted in the 2x2 grid also don't have custom SkyBlock lore or rarity tags, and can't be stacked with items that do.
To fix this bug, simply throw the item(s) without the tag on the ground with other items with it so they stack, then pick them back up again.
## Quick Crafting
Quick Crafting is a mechanic unlocked at [[Carpentry|Carpentry III (3)]]. It was added in the 0.7.6 update, and it allows players to quickly craft multiple items. There are three slots to the right of the output slot in the Crafting UI where the three most expensive items that can be crafted with the materials in the player's inventory will appear. This feature is only available to players with the 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] rank and above. On [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profiles, it is available to all players, including those without a rank.
## Supercrafting
Supercrafting is a mechanic only available to players with 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] and above. It allows crafting specific amounts of items by using ingredients directly from the player's [[Sacks]], [[Ender Chest]], [[Backpack|Backpacks]], and other inventories.
## Recipe
One of the most common crafting recipes in SkyBlock is Enchanted items.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|Crafting recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|Cobblestone IV (4) ([[Cobblestone]])|160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|
It usually requires 160 of the raw materials to craft their enchanted equivalent. An exception to this are items that do not stack up to 64. For example, since [[Ender Pearl]] only stack up to 16, their enchanted form, [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]], has a different crafting recipe that requires 20 Ender Pearls. Another exception is with [[Enchanted Hard Stone|Enchanted Hardstone]] requiring 576 [[Hard Stone|Hardstone]] to craft.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|Crafting recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Ender Pearl]]|Ender Pearl II (2) ([[Ender Pearl]])|20x [[Ender Pearl]]|Recipe: [[Ender Pearl]] x4 ×7 → [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]]|
### Forms
There are two forms of recipes.
Normal recipes are crafted in one or more fixed configurations (or pre-defined positions). To craft an item with a normal recipe, the amounts and positions of ingredients must align with one of the pre-defined positions.
For example, similar to most "Enchanted" tier of items crafted with 160 of the previous tier item, the crafting recipe for [[Enchanted Diamond]] is a normal recipe with 2 fixed configurations.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|Crafting recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Diamond]]|Diamond IV (4) ([[Diamond]])|160x [[Diamond]]|Recipe: [[Diamond]] x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Diamond]]|
|[[Enchanted Diamond]]|Diamond IV (4) ([[Diamond]])|160x [[Block of Diamond]]|Recipe: [[Block of Diamond]] x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Diamond]] x9|
Shapeless recipes are crafted with no fixed configurations. To craft an item with a shapeless recipe, the amounts of ingredients must match the recipe, with no requirement on the positions of ingredients. When crafting, items can be rearranged in any way and still create the same item.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Crafting.|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added Quick Crafting.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Added item lore to Quick Crafting.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed shapeless recipes taking away too many ingredients in some cases.|
Linked from: [["Fragranced" Brown Mushroom "Paste"]], [[2 IQ Points]], [[Absolute Ender Pearl]], [[Abysmal Lasso]], [[Abyssal Armor]], [[Acacia Birdhouse]], [[Acacia Log]], [[Acacia Minion XII Upgrade Stone]], [[Accessories/Dungeons Loot]], [[Accessories/Obtained From Collections or Recipes]], [[Accessories/Slayer Recipes]], [[Accretion Artifact]], [[Accretion Ring]], [[Accretion Talisman]], [[Activator Rail]], [[Adaptive Armor]], [[Adaptive Belt]], [[Adaptive Blade]], [[Agaricus Cap]], [[Agaricus Cap Bunch]], [[Agaricus Cap Cap]], [[Agaricus Chumcap]], [[Agaricus Soup]], [[Agarimoo Artifact]], [[Agarimoo Ring]], [[Agarimoo Talisman]], [[Agarimoo Tongue]], [[Aggourdian]], [[Agronomy Sack]], [[Alert Flare]], [[All Skills Exp Boost]], [[All Skills Exp Super-Boost]], [[All-in Aloe]], [[All-in Aloe Fragment]], [[Alligator Skin]], [[Amalgamated Crimsonite]], [[Ancestral Spade]], [[Ancient Claw]], [[Ancient Cloak]], [[Ancient Rose]] (+1746 more)

The Crafting Table is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Vanilla block that can be used to craft. Hypixel SkyBlock uses a custom GUI for all crafting purposes. Using the Crafting Table will level up your [[Carpentry]] skill.
You can craft a Crafting Table then place it on your island — or alternatively access it in your [[SkyBlock Menu]].
If you haven't unlocked a recipe, the Crafting Table will not give you the crafted item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|4 [[Planks]]|Recipe: [[Planks]] ×4 → Crafting Table|

### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Carpentry Table]]|Unlock [[Carpentry]]|[[Crafting Table]] 2x Crafting Table 4x [[Stick]] 10x Oak Planks ([[Planks]])|Recipe: [[Planks]] x2 ×5 + Crafting Table x2 + [[Stick]] x2 ×2 → [[Carpentry Table]]|
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Attempting to place Crafting Table on public islands|You can't place blocks on public islands! A Crafting Table can be accessed via your SkyBlock Menu or with /craft!|
## Trivia
- Because the SkyBlock Crafting Table uses the chest GUI, you can shift-click items into it, unlike older Vanilla versions.

|[[Enchanted Charcoal]]|Coal V (5) ([[Coal]])|32 Log ([[Wood (disambiguation)]]) (Vanilla) 128 (2 stacks) [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] x32 ×6 + [[Oak Log]] x32/[[Birch Log]] x32/[[Spruce Log]] x32/Dark Oak Log x32/[[Acacia Log]] x32/[[Jungle Log]] x32 → [[Enchanted Charcoal]]|
|[[Roofed Forest Island]]|Dark Oak Log IV (4)|[[Dark Oak Log]] 15x Dark Oak Log 30x [[Dirt]]|Recipe: Dark Oak Log x5 ×3 + [[Dirt]] x5 ×6 → [[Roofed Forest Island]]|
|Dark Oak Minion I ([[Dark Oak Minion]])|Dark Oak Log I (1)|1x [[Wooden Axe]] [[Dark Oak Log]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Dark Oak Log|Recipe: Dark Oak Log x10 ×8 + [[Wooden Axe]] → [[Dark Oak Minion]]|
|Dark Oak Minion II ([[Dark Oak Minion]])|Dark Oak Log I (1)|1x Dark Oak Minion I ([[Dark Oak Minion]]) [[Dark Oak Log]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Dark Oak Log|Recipe: Dark Oak Log x20 ×8 + [[Dark Oak Minion]] → [[Dark Oak Minion]]|
|Dark Oak Minion III ([[Dark Oak Minion]])|Dark Oak Log I (1)|1x Dark Oak Minion II ([[Dark Oak Minion]]) [[Dark Oak Log]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) Dark Oak Log|Recipe: Dark Oak Log x40 ×8 + [[Dark Oak Minion]] → [[Dark Oak Minion]]|
|Dark Oak Minion IV ([[Dark Oak Minion]])|Dark Oak Log I (1)|1x Dark Oak Minion III ([[Dark Oak Minion]]) [[Dark Oak Log]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Dark Oak Log|Recipe: Dark Oak Log x64 ×8 + [[Dark Oak Minion]] → [[Dark Oak Minion]]|
|Dark Oak Planks ([[Planks]])|None|[[Dark Oak Log]] 1x Dark Oak Log|Recipe: Dark Oak Log → [[Planks]] x4|
|[[Bronze Ship Helm]]|None|4x [[Bronze Bowl]] [[Dark Oak Log]] 64x Dark Oak Log|Recipe: [[Bronze Bowl]] ×4 + Dark Oak Log x64 → [[Bronze Ship Helm]]|
### [[Trials of Fire]]
[[Dark Oak Log]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) Dark Oak Logs are required to upgrade the [[Campfire Badge|Campfire Initiate Badge]] to the [[Campfire Badge|Campfire Adept Badge]] after completing Tier 4 of the [[Trials of Fire]].

Dark Prismarine is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block that can be purchased from the [[Builder]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Dark Prismarine can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 10 [[Coins]].
### Pond Island
The [[Pond Island]] can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]], which comes with 27 Dark Prismarine in the structure.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Ink Sac]] 8x [[Prismarine Shard]]|Recipe: [[Prismarine Shard]] ×8 + [[Ink Sac]] → Dark Prismarine|
## Usage
### Private Island
Dark Prismarine can be placed as a decorative block on the player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]].
### Mithril Ore
Breakable Dark Prismarine on [[Mining Islands]] represents [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]], which has a [[Block Strength]] of 800 and can be broken to obtain 2 [[Mithril]] and 3 [[Mithril Powder]].

### Crafting

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIAMOND
Tooltip: Collection Item; COMMON
Diamond is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Collections|Collection]] item in the [[Mining]] category. It can be obtained by mining in the [[Diamond Reserve]], [[Obsidian Sanctuary]], [[Dwarven Mines]], and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Collection
Collecting diamonds increases the player's Diamond Collection, which grants the player useful items and perks upon collecting a certain amount of diamonds.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Diamond Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Execute]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|III|250|Portal to the Deep Caverns ([[Portals]]) Recipe|+4|
|IV|1,000|[[Enchanted Diamond]] Recipe|+4|
|V|2,500|[[Critical]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|VI|5,000|[[Diamond Spreading]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|10,000|[[Hardened Diamond Armor]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|25,000|[[Enchanted Diamond Block]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|50,000|[[Perfect Armor]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Natural Generation
Diamond can be obtained from mining two different blocks, [[Ores|Diamond Ore]], and [[Ores|Pure Diamond]]. Mining Diamond Ore grants +12 [[Mining|Mining XP]], whilst mining Pure Diamond grants +15.
In [[Deep Caverns]] it can be found in two locations. It is present in the [[Diamond Reserve]] in ore form only and in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] in both ore and pure forms.
In the [[Dwarven Mines]], Diamond Ore can be found in some small decorative patches around the island, and Pure Diamond can be found in bigger quantities in the lower parts of the [[Royal Mines]].
In the [[Crystal Hollows]], Diamond Ore can be found throughout the map, and Pure Diamond can be found in [[Mines of Divan]].
### Minions
Diamonds can also be obtained through the use of a [[Diamond Minion]], [[Revenant Minion]] and the [[Diamond Spreading]] minion upgrade on the [[Private Island]].
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|

# Dirt
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Crafting ingredients
Dirt is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Dirt.
[Dirt]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIRT
Tooltip: COMMON
Dirt is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block that can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
## Obtaining
### Natural Generation
Dirt can be found and harvested on the player's starting [[Private Island]] and plots in [[The Garden]]. It can also be spawned from the [[Farming Island]] and [[Roofed Forest Island]].
It can also be found and destroyed in the [[Crystal Hollows]], although this will not grant dirt.
### Trades
2 Dirt can be traded in exchange for 8 [[Seeds]] in the [[Trades]] menu after unlocking [[Seeds|Seeds I (1)]].
### Shop Purchase
Dirt can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 2 [[Coins]].
### Dirt Rod
The [[Dirt Rod]] can be used to fish up Dirt.
### Starter Chest
5 Dirt can be found in the starting [[Chest]] that spawns on a new [[Private Island]].
## Usage
### Private Island
Dirt can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Farming Island]]|Wheat VI (6) ([[Wheat]])|[[Dirt]] 30x Dirt 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Wheat]] x32 ×3 + Dirt x5 ×6 → [[Farming Island]]|
|[[Roofed Forest Island]]|Dark Oak Log IV (4) ([[Dark Oak Log]])|15x [[Dark Oak Log]] [[Dirt]] 30x Dirt|Recipe: [[Dark Oak Log]] x5 ×3 + Dirt x5 ×6 → [[Roofed Forest Island]]|
|[[Coarse Dirt]]|None|[[Dirt]] 2x Dirt 2x [[Gravel]]|Recipe: Dirt ×2 + [[Gravel]] ×2 → [[Coarse Dirt]] x4|
### Minions
Dirt can be used as a surface material on the player's [[Private Island]] for various [[Minions]].
### Trades
Dirt can used in the [[Trades]] menu to obtain several items.
|Requirement|Item|Cost|
|-|-|-|
|None|4 [[Grass]]|[[Dirt]] 4 Dirt|
|Seeds III (3) ([[Seeds]])|[[Long Grass]]|[[Dirt]] 8 Dirt|
|Seeds IV (4) ([[Seeds]])|[[Fern]]|[[Dirt]] 8 Dirt|
|Seeds V (5) ([[Seeds]])|[[Dead Bush]]|[[Dirt]] 8 Dirt|
|Seeds VI (6) ([[Seeds]])|[[Double Tallgrass]]|[[Dirt]] 8 Dirt|
## History

A Dispenser is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Vanilla Objects/Redstone-Related Blocks|Redstone-related]] block that can be used on the player's [[Private Island]]. It works the same as a [[Dropper]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Redstone Dust]] 1x Bow ([[Bows]]) 7x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] ×7 + [[Bows]] + [[Redstone Dust]] → Dispenser|
## Usage
### Private Island
Dispensers can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]]. When activated from a [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]] signal, it will act like a [[Dropper]] and drop an item from its inventory, rather than dispense one. There are also [[Private Island|restrictions]] on how often this can be done. Dispensers cannot be placed in [[The Garden]].

|-|-|
|You can't use that here!|Trying to place a Dispenser in [[The Garden]]|

One Double Tallgrass can be obtained in exchange for 8 [[Dirt]] in the [[Trades]] menu, after unlocking [[Seeds|Seeds VI (6)]].

### Private Island

### Minions
Eggs can be obtained from [[Chicken|Chickens]] spawned from [[Chicken Minion|Chicken Minions]]. An [[Enchanted Egg]] guarantees an Egg to be collected upon spawning.
### Laid Eggs
The [[Chicken|Chickens]] in [[The Barn]] and the Chicken next to [[Gulliver]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]] will occasionally lay an Egg.
### Chicken Hat
Crouching while wearing a [[Chicken Hat]] lays an Egg.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Stone Platform]]|Cobblestone II (2) ([[Cobblestone]])|[[Egg]] 1x Egg 64x [[Stone Bricks]]|Recipe: [[Stone Bricks]] x8 ×8 + Egg → [[Stone Platform]]|
|[[Enchanted Egg]]|Raw Chicken V (5) ([[Raw Chicken]])|[[Egg]] 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) Egg|Recipe: Egg x16 ×9 → [[Enchanted Egg]]|
|[[Chicken Hat]]|Raw Chicken III (3) ([[Raw Chicken]])|[[Egg]] 5x Egg|Recipe: Egg ×8 → [[Chicken Hat]]|
|[[Bridge Egg]]|Raw Chicken II (2) ([[Raw Chicken]])|[[Egg]] 5x Egg 8x [[Stone]]|Recipe: [[Stone]] ×8 + Egg x5 → [[Bridge Egg]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Horse Pet]]|Leather III (3) ([[Leather]])|[[Egg]] 1x Egg 64x [[Leather]]|Recipe: [[Leather]] x8 ×8 + Egg → [[Horse Pet]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Chicken Pet]]|Raw Chicken III (3) ([[Raw Chicken]])|[[Egg]] 1x Egg 64x [[Raw Chicken]]|Recipe: [[Raw Chicken]] x8 ×8 + Egg → [[Chicken Pet]]|
|[[Cake]]|None|[[Egg]] 1x Egg 2x [[Sugar]] 3x [[Milk Bucket]] 3x [[Wheat]]|Recipe: [[Milk Bucket]] ×3 + [[Sugar]] ×2 + Egg + [[Wheat]] ×3 → [[Cake]]|
|[[Pumpkin Pie]]|None|1x [[Pumpkin]] 1x [[Sugar]] [[Egg]] 1x Egg|Recipe: [[Pumpkin]] + [[Sugar]] + Egg → [[Pumpkin Pie]]|

## Collection
Collecting emeralds increases the player's emerald [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of emeralds.

# Enchanted Golden Apple
Categories: Vanilla objects, Uncommon items, Items, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Enchanted Golden Apple is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Enchanted Golden Apple.
[Enchanted Golden Apple]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]

An Enchanted Golden Apple is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be consumed or used to craft the [[Saving Grace]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Apple]] 8x [[Block of Gold]]|Recipe: [[Block of Gold]] ×8 + [[Apple]] → Enchanted Golden Apple|
## Usage
### Consuming
An Enchanted Golden Apple grants [[Resistance Potion|Resistance I]] and [[Fire Resistance Potion|Fire Resistance I]] for 5 minutes, [[Absorption Potion|Absorption IV]] for 2 minutes and [[Regeneration Potion|Regeneration II]] for 20 seconds when consumed.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Saving Grace]]|Ender Pearl IX (9) ([[Ender Pearl]])|[[Enchanted Golden Apple]] 16x Enchanted Golden Apple 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] x16 ×8 + Enchanted Golden Apple x16 → [[Saving Grace]]|
## Trivia
- The recipe for crafting aEnchanted Golden Apple was removed in the Minecraft Java version 1.9, but is still available in SkyBlock.

# Enchantment Table
Aliases: Bookshelf Power, Enchanting Table
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Blocks, Craftable items, Pages with UIs
Enchantment Table is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Enchantment Table.
[Enchantment Table]
Type: Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Uses: [[Enchanting]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 40 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_TABLE
Tooltip: COMMON
Enchantment Tables are a block that allows the [[Player]] to enchant items.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Book]] 2x [[Diamond]] 4x [[Obsidian]]|Recipe: [[Book]] + [[Diamond]] ×2 + [[Obsidian]] ×4 → Enchantment Table|
## Usage
The Enchantment Table can be used to add certain low-tier [[Enchantments]] on items.
It can also be used to remove [[Enchantments]], including those applied via [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] rather than the table, [[Stacking Enchantments]] and [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrades]].
## Trivia
- Unlike vanilla version, Enchantment Table in SkyBlock doesn't require any [[Bookshelf|Bookshelves]] nearby to get better enchantments after the 0.26.1 update.
- Enchantment Table can be accessed with /et, /etable, /enchantingtable, /enchantingtablemenu, /enchantmenttable or /enchantmenttablemenu commands while the [[Booster Cookie]] is active.
- The maximum theoretically achievable Bookshelf Power was 480 with 32 [[Enchanted Bookshelf]] blocks placed down.
  - However, there was no point in going above 20.
- On [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profiles, the Enchantment Table in the Hub used to have 2,147,483,647 Bookshelf Power.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 15, 2026|Removed Bookshelf Power.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Enchantment Table.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Reworked Enchantment Table.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Enchantment Table can now remove enchantments.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Enchantment Table now has a full list of enchantments instead of 1-3 random ones.|
|Unknown Date|Temporarily, the Enchantment Table can not be placed down.|
|Jan 7, 2022|The Enchantment Table can now be placed again.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|All Enchantment ([[Enchantments]]) tiers can now be removed in [[The Hex]] and in the Enchantment Table.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Removed Bookshelf Power.|

# Ender Chest
Aliases: Ender Chest Lv1, Ender Chest Lv2, Ender Chest Lv3, Enderchest, Enderchest Upgrade
Categories: Vanilla objects, Pages with UIs, Upgrades, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu, Storages
Ender Chest is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Ender Chest.
[Ender Chest]
Type: Storage upgrade
Appearance: Ender Chest
Usage: Storage for anything
[Capacity]
Default: 54 slots
The Ender Chest is a storage feature that allows players to store items that can be accessed globally. It can be accessed in the [[SkyBlock Menu]], interacting with a physical [[Ender Chest]] Ender Chest, or through the /enderchest or /ec commands.
By default, the Ender Chest only has 54 slots for items, but can be upgraded at the [[Elizabeth|Community Shop]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Upgrades
|Upgrade|Total slots|Additional slots|Unlocking|
|-|-|-|-|
|Default|54||Unlocked by default|
|Level 1|99|+45|Community Shop ([[Elizabeth]]) (Free)|
|Level 2|144|+45|Community Shop ([[Elizabeth]]) (75 Gems)|
|Level 3|189|+45|Community Shop ([[Elizabeth]]) (150 Gems)|
|Level 4|234|+45|Community Shop ([[Elizabeth]]) (150 Gems)|
|Level 5|279|+45|Community Shop ([[Elizabeth]]) (150 Gems)|
|Level 6|324|+45|Community Shop ([[Elizabeth]]) (150 Gems)|
|Level 7|369|+45|Community Shop ([[Elizabeth]]) (150 Gems)|
|Level 8|405|+36|Community Shop ([[Elizabeth]]) (150 Gems)|
## Usage
The Ender Chest is used to store items of any kind, except items that can store other items (e.g. [[Builder's Wand]]). This can be useful if the player's inventory fills up often from grinding. It can be accessed from anywhere, and can be upgraded to have up to 9 pages (405 slots, or 25,920 items in stacks of 64).

# Ender Crystal
Aliases: End Crystal
Categories: Vanilla objects, The End
Ender Crystal is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Ender Crystal.
[Ender Crystal]
The Ender Crystal is an object found in [[The End]]. They only spawn during [[Ender Dragon]] boss fights to gradually heal the dragon. To destroy an Ender Crystal, the player needs to hit the crystal with a bow or melee weapon. They will respawn a certain amount of time after being destroyed. Destroying them will cause them to drop a [[Crystal Fragment]], which is used to craft the [[Catalyst]] and the [[Crystal Armor]].
## Drops
- [[Crystal Fragment]]
## Chat Messages
☬ An Ender Crystal has respawned! - When a Ender Crystal respawns
☬ <player> has destroyed an Ender Crystal! - When a player destroys an Ender Crystal
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Ender Crystal.|
