# Ender Dragon
Aliases: Dragon, Dragon Weight, Dragons, End Dragon, End Dragons, Ender Dragon/Drop Tables, Ender dragon, Enderdragon, Old Dragon, Primal Dragon, Protector Dragon, Strong Dragon, Superior Dragon, Unstable Dragon, Wise Dragon, Young Dragon
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Dragon Armor, The End
Ender Dragon is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Ender Dragon.
[Ender Dragon]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 325-367
Spawn Location: [[Dragon's Nest]]
Mob Type: [[Ender]] [[Arcane]] [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 7,500,000-15,000,000; [[Damage]]: 1,100-2,200; [[True Damage]]: 30%-70% of player's [[Health]]
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Ender Dragon Pet]] 0–1x [[Aspect of the Dragons]] 0–1x [[Dragon Armor]] 3–32x [[Dragon Fragment]] 0–4x [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] 10–16x [[Ender Pearl]] 0–1x [[Dragon Scale]] 0–1x [[Dragon Claw]] 0–1x [[Dragon Horn]] 0–1x [[Travel Scroll to Dragon's Nest]]
XP: 2,437-2,752 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 975-1101
[Metadata]
ID: PROTECTOR_DRAGON_100; OLD_DRAGON_100; WISE_DRAGON_100; UNSTABLE_DRAGON_100; YOUNG_DRAGON_100; STRONG_DRAGON_100; SUPERIOR_DRAGON_100
Ender Dragons are 7 different variants of [[Bosses|Boss]] summoned with [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] in the [[Dragon's Nest]]. They drop powerful loot including [[Dragon Armor|Dragon Armors]] and [[Dragon Fragment|Dragon Fragments]] when killed.
The Ender Dragon has 7 variants: Protector, Wise, Unstable, Superior, Strong, Young and Old.
## Mechanics
### Summoning
The Ender Dragon is summoned with 8 [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]]. These eyes must be placed in the altar in the center of the nest. After all 8 are placed, the dragon is summoned and the fight will begin. This is similar to the summoning of [[Arachne]] with [[Arachne's Calling|Arachne's Callings]].
### Variants
The Ender Dragon will spawn as one of seven variants. The dragon's variant affects the its health, behavior, drops, and attacks.
|Level|Name|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Defense]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Stats [[Speed]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv333]|Protector Dragon|16%|9,000,000|85|1,100|140%|2,497|999|832|PROTECTOR_DRAGON_100|
|[Lv358]|Old Dragon|16%|15,000,000|18|1,100|130%|2,685|1,074|895|OLD_DRAGON_100|
|[Lv367]|Wise Dragon|16%|9,000,000|25|2,200|140%|2,752|1,101|917|WISE_DRAGON_100|
|[Lv333]|Unstable Dragon|16%|9,000,000|25|1,100|140%|2,497|999|832|UNSTABLE_DRAGON_100|
|[Lv325]|Young Dragon|16%|7,500,000|18|1,100|225%|2,437|975|812|YOUNG_DRAGON_100|
|[Lv333]|Strong Dragon|16%|9,000,000|25|1,100|140%|2,497|999|832|STRONG_DRAGON_100|
|[Lv367]|Superior Dragon|4%*|12,000,000|30|1,650|180%|2,752|1,101|917|SUPERIOR_DRAGON_100|
For Superior Dragons, [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]] [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] increase the chance of spawning a Superior Dragon, starting at 12.5% with 1 recombobulated eye and up to 100% with 8 recombobulated eyes.
Each dragon also has their own abilities and fly height:
|Dragon|Y-Level; Fly Height|Abilities and Notes|
|-|-|-|
|Protector Dragon|40|Protective Scales: Takes reduced damage from all sources|
|Old Dragon|30|Old Bulkiness: Highest health of all dragons but also the slowest of them all as well.|
|Wise Dragon|40|Wise Caster: Fireball and Lightning attacks occur more frequently Fireball and Lightning deal +20% damage|
|Unstable Dragon|40|Unstable Energy: Sometimes damages players when approaching the dragon, dealing 3,000 [[Damage]]. Implosion: Has a chance to explode upon death, dealing 2,000 [[Damage]] to players caught in the blast. Grants an achievement for surviving the blast|
|Young Dragon|100|Young Agility: Has the highest speed of all dragons but also has the lowest health of them all as well.|
|Strong Dragon|40|Strong Spells: Rush, Fireball and Lightning deals more damage Rush and Contact deal +20% damage|
|Superior Dragon|65|Superiority: Has all the abilities of the six previous dragons at a weaker degree. Rarest of all dragons Rush and Contact deal +15% damage Fireball and Lightning deal +15% damage|
### The Dragon Fight
After 8 [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] have been placed, there will be a brief period before the dragon will hatch from an egg in the sky, knocking all players a large distance away from the Dragon Altar at the center of the [[Dragon's Nest]]. After the dragon has spawned, the dragon variant will be announced in the chat.
The door to the [[Dragon's Nest]] will shut, the gaps at the top will be covered with glass and 5 [[Ender Crystal|Ender Crystals]] will spawn around the nest on top of obsidian spikes.
Additionally, [[Gregory]] the Opportunist will spawn near the [[Dragon's Nest|Dragon's Gate]]. He will sell a [[Decent Bow]] as well as Arrows at a higher price than the [[Weaponsmith]] in town.
#### Ender Crystals
At the start of a Dragon Fight, 6 Ender Crystals will spawn in 6 of 10 locations. Ender Crystals cause the dragon to heal +0.3% [[Health]] per second if they are connected to the dragon. Every tick, there is a 10% chance for the crystals' connections to the dragon to be refreshed. If the dragon is within 32 blocks of any crystal, the crystal begins to heal it. Destroying a crystal rewards its destroyer with 1 [[Crystal Fragment]] and 1,000 Dragon [[Damage]] contributed. Destroyed crystals will respawn after 1-2 minutes, and can only respawn 3 times.
### Attacks
The dragon has three attacks, which all deal [[True Damage]]. When under 50% [[Health]], they deal 50% more damage.
1. Rush - The dragon will charge a player, inflicting 2,000 [[Damage]], 550 [[True Damage]], and [[Knockback]].
2. Fireball - The dragon will fly to a point in the arena, stop in place, then fire a barrage of fireballs at players. These fireballs explode and can damage multiple players at once. They will deal 1,700 [[Damage]], 200 [[True Damage]], and knock players high up into the air.
3. Lightning - The dragon will briefly stop in place, summoning lightning around it and creating audible thunder sounds. After a brief period, it will summon lightning and smite players from above, dealing 1,700 [[Damage]] and 200 [[True Damage]]. This attack can be avoided if the player has a block above their head.
If the player comes into contact with the dragon, they will receive 50% of their [[Health]] + 2,000 in [[Damage]].
After some time has passed, the dragon will summon groups of [[Zealot|Zealots]]. These [[Zealot|Zealots]] are immediately hostile, even if the player has not damaged or looked at them. The Dragon also makes already existing Zealots hostile.
When players are killed, they must make their way down to the [[Dragon's Nest]] again, and break down the sealed door. The door has lots of health, can be attacked with both ranged and melee attacks and will open again when its health bar is depleted. Both endermites and endermen spawn as the door takes damage. This can be avoided by using a [[Rat Pet]], [[Armadillo Pet]], or [[Travel Scrolls|Dragon's Nest Travel Scroll]].
After the Ender Dragon is defeated, [[Gregory]] will depart and hostile mobs will not spawn in the [[Dragon's Nest]] for a brief amount of time. All existing mobs within the Nest, including [[Voidgloom Seraph]] and [[Necromancy]] mobs, will instantly despawn. During this time, dragons cannot be summoned. After this brief time has passed, a message in the chat will announce the respawning of the Dragon Egg, after which mobs will spawn as normal and players are able to summon another dragon.

### Quality System
Each player that damages the Ender Dragon will receive individual loot, including [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] and [[Enchanted Ender Pearl|Enchanted Ender Pearls]]. Loot received is governed by Loot Quality (commonly incorrectly referred to as "Weight" by players), which determines a player's eligibility for major loot. Note that achieving a quality requirement does not guarantee the respective drop.
Total Loot Quality is calculated using this formula: Quality=BaseQuality+100(SummoningEyesPlaced)+100(PlayerDamageDealtFirstPlaceDamageDealt)
Base Loot Quality is determined in the following manner:
|Condition|Quality|
|-|-|
|Placing Summoning Eyes ([[Summoning Eye]])|+100 Loot Quality per eye placed (up to 800 quality from 8 eyes)|
|Dealing Damage|100 x (Player's Damage ÷ First Place Player's Damage) Loot Quality|
|1st in Damage Dealt|+300 Loot Quality|
|2nd in Damage Dealt|+250 Loot Quality|
|3rd in Damage Dealt|+200 Loot Quality|
|4th in Damage Dealt|+125 Loot Quality|
|5th in Damage Dealt|+110 Loot Quality|
|6-8th in Damage Dealt|+100 Loot Quality|
|9-10th in Damage Dealt|+90 Loot Quality|
|11-12th in Damage Dealt|+80 Loot Quality|
|13-25th in Damage Dealt|+70 Loot Quality|
|More than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|+70 Loot Quality|
|Less than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|+10 Loot Quality|
The player will receive a guaranteed quantity of drops, depending on their damage placement.
|Condition| [[Ender Pearl]]| [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]]|
|-|-|-|
|1st in Damage Dealt|30|7|
|2nd in Damage Dealt|25|6|
|3rd in Damage Dealt|22|5|
|4th in Damage Dealt|20|4|
|5th in Damage Dealt|18|3|
|6-8th in Damage Dealt|15|2|
|9-10th in Damage Dealt|15|2|
|11-12th in Damage Dealt|15|2|
|13-18th in Damage Dealt|12|1|
|19-25th in Damage Dealt|10|0|
|Below 25th in Damage Dealt More than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|8|0|
|Below 25th in Damage Dealt Less than 1 [[Damage]] dealt|5|0|
The player can obtain certain drops based off their total Loot Quality. After receiving a major drop, the player's Loot Quality is recalculated: Quality=Quality−DropQuality, and the player will not be able to receive any other pieces of major loot. Drops are affected by [[Magic Find]] and [[Pet Luck]].
Major Loot:
|Slot|Item|Quality Requirement|Base Drop Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ender Dragon Pet]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Ender Dragon Pet]]|450 Loot Quality|Summoning Eyes placed by player × 0.01%|
|[[Ender Dragon Pet]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Ender Dragon Pet]]|449 Loot Quality|Summoning Eyes placed by player × 0.05%|
|[[Dragon Horn]]|[[Dragon Horn]]|448 Loot Quality|30% (Superior Dragon only)|
|[[Aspect of the Dragons]]|[[Aspect of the Dragons]]|447 Loot Quality|Summoning Eyes placed by player × 3%; (Non-Superior Dragons only)|
|[[Dragon Claw]]|[[Dragon Claw]]|446 Loot Quality|Summoning Eyes placed by player × 2%|
|[[Protector Dragon Armor]]/[[Old Dragon Armor]]/[[Unstable Dragon Armor]]/[[Wise Dragon Armor]]/[[Young Dragon Armor]]/[[Strong Dragon Armor]]/[[Superior Dragon Armor]]|Dragon Chestplate ([[Dragon Armor]])|410 Loot Quality|30%|
|[[Protector Dragon Armor]]/[[Old Dragon Armor]]/[[Unstable Dragon Armor]]/[[Wise Dragon Armor]]/[[Young Dragon Armor]]/[[Strong Dragon Armor]]/[[Superior Dragon Armor]]|Dragon Leggings ([[Dragon Armor]])|360 Loot Quality|30%|
|[[Dragon Scale]]|[[Dragon Scale]]|296 Loot Quality|30% (Young Dragon only)|
|[[Protector Dragon Armor]]/[[Old Dragon Armor]]/[[Unstable Dragon Armor]]/[[Wise Dragon Armor]]/[[Young Dragon Armor]]/[[Strong Dragon Armor]]/[[Superior Dragon Armor]]|Dragon Helmet ([[Dragon Armor]])|295 Loot Quality|30%|
|[[Protector Dragon Armor]]/[[Old Dragon Armor]]/[[Unstable Dragon Armor]]/[[Wise Dragon Armor]]/[[Young Dragon Armor]]/[[Strong Dragon Armor]]/[[Superior Dragon Armor]]|Dragon Boots ([[Dragon Armor]])|290 Loot Quality|30%|
|[[Travel Scroll to Dragon's Nest]]|[[Travel Scroll to Dragon's Nest]]|250 Loot Quality|35% (Unstable Dragon only)|
After all possible pieces of major loot are rolled, the remaining quality is converted to Dragon Fragments, Enchanted Ender Pearls, and Ender Pearls, in the order of highest quality requirement to lowest:
Other Drops:
|Slot|Item|Quality Requirement|
|-|-|-|
|[[Dragon Fragment]]|[[Dragon Fragment]]|22 Loot Quality|
|[[Enchanted Ender Pearl]]|[[Enchanted Ender Pearl]]|15 Loot Quality|
|[[Ender Pearl]]|[[Ender Pearl]]|5 Loot Quality|
Additionally, Ender Dragon has drops from a pool that rolls separately from other pools (multiple different items from it can be obtained at the same time):
Additional Loot:
|Slot|Item|Quality Requirement|Base Drop Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Pearlescent Dye]]|[[Pearlescent Dye]]|N/A|0.001% (0.002% from Superior Dragon)|
|[[Draconic Shard]]|[[Draconic Shard]]|125 Loot Quality; (not deducted)|100%|
|[[Dragon Essence]]|5 [[Dragon Essence]]; (10x from Superior Dragon)|N/A|100%|

## Tips
- The [[Dragon Shortbow]] is a relatively effective early game weapon to use for Dragon fights as it is easy to obtain (through the [[Lone Adventurer]]'s quest) and deals decent damage.
- Due to the price of [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]], summoning Ender Dragons is generally not a good idea, as it commonly results in a loss of [[Coins]].
- The [[Draconic Talisman]], [[Draconic Ring]], and [[Draconic Artifact]] increase damage dealt to Dragons by 2%, 3%, and 5% respectively.
- Placing 4 [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] and dealing at least 1 damage will make you eligible for the whole loot table of the Ender Dragon.
  - This is why it is recommended to only use 4 [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] yourself, unless you are specifically looking for the [[Ender Dragon Pet]], [[Aspect of the Dragons]] or [[Dragon Claw]], where the amount of [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] used will affect the chance of these items dropping.
## Trivia
- Similar to other bosses, players will see their ranking for how much damage they dealt to the boss.
- The Ender Dragon was the second Boss added to the game.
- There is currently a bug in which in certain lobbies, summoned dragons will disappear after a player hits it with the [[Aspect of the Dragons]] ability. The boss bar and [[Gregory]] are still present, but the dragon itself is missing. [[Ender Crystal|Ender Crystals]] still spawn around a minute after the disappearance, and it stays like that until the lobby resets.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- When a Dragon is defeated, mobs, including [[Voidgloom Seraph|Voidgloom Seraphs]], in the lower areas of the [[Void Sepulture]] (between layers 10 and 26) will also despawn.[Confirm]
- Holy Dragons do not exist, even though [[Holy Dragon Armor]], [[Dragon Fragment|Holy Dragon Fragments]], and Holy [[Lost Adventurer|Lost Adventurers]] do.

### Minions
Ender Pearls can be obtained through [[Enderman Minion|Enderman Minions]].
## Usage
### Throwing
Ender Pearls can also be thrown to teleport with. Unlike vanilla Minecraft, Ender Pearls in SkyBlock use custom mechanics, teleporting the player to the center of the target block server-side, similarly to the [[Aspect of the End]]. There are also further restrictions on their use, such as not being able to throw a pearl if aiming at a block within the player's reach, unless the player has build permission (which for SkyBlock generally entails the [[Private Island]] and [[The Garden]]).
Throwing more than one Ender Pearl on [[The End]] will anger nearby [[Enderman|Endermen]] and cause them to attack the player. This resets after a few minutes, and the next pearl thrown will once again show a warning message. [[Silent Pearl|Silent Pearls]] can be used instead to avoid angering Endermen.

## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|First pearl thrown on [[The End]]|Be careful! Using Ender Pearls on this island will anger nearby Endermen!|
## Tips
- Ender Pearls can be used to obtain some [[Fairy Souls]] more easily.
## Trivia
- The death message when a player dies to Endermen angered by throwing Ender Pearls is simply "[player] died".
- In the minion, the pearls will stack to 64. However, when collected into the inventory, the pearls will be placed in stacks of 16. This may result in the inventory getting clogged up when collecting from the [[Enderman Minion|Enderman Minions]], since each stack of pearls in the minion takes up 4 slots in the inventory. However, this causes the minion to have more storage.

The Enderman is a Neutral Mob that spawns in [[The End]].
## Spawning
Endermen can be found in [[The End]]. Endermen become stronger the lower they spawn.
## Variants
|Level|Spawn Location|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|

|[Lv45]|[[The End]]|4,500|500|67|49|15|ENDERMAN_42|
|[Lv51]|[[The End]]|6,000|600|76|56|17|ENDERMAN_45|
|[Lv59]|[[The End]]|9,000|700|88|64|19|ENDERMAN_50|
## Behavior
Endermen in SkyBlock act almost exactly like Endermen in vanilla Minecraft. They are passive when spawned, and will not attack the player, instead choosing to walk around the area they spawned into randomly. If the player attacks them or looks directly at their head, they will become aggressive, chasing the player around until they touch water, despawn, or die. In SkyBlock and in [[The End]], Endermen can also become agitated when throwing an [[Ender Pearl]], but not a [[Silent Pearl]]
Endermen cannot be attacked using a [[Bows|Bow]] or [[Shortbows|Shortbow]] other than the [[Juju Shortbow]] or [[Terminator]].

## Trivia
- Wearing a [[Pumpkin]] or [[Enderman Mask]] will prevent angering Endermen by looking at them.
- Similar to in vanilla Minecraft, Endermen are immune to bows. If hit with an arrow, they won't take damage. Several bows are still able to deal damage to them, most notably the [[Juju Shortbow]] and [[Terminator]].
- When an Enderman takes damage from water, the damage number is blue.
## Bugs
- Killing a [[Private Island]] Enderman may result in [[The End]] Enderman bestiary being copied over.

# Endermite
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, The End
Endermite is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Endermite.
[Endermite]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 34-37
Spawn Location: [[The End]] [[Dragon's Nest]]
Mob Type: [[Arthropod]] [[Ender]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 2,000-2,300; [[Damage]]: 400-475
[Drops]
Drops: 1–4x [[End Stone]] 0–1x [[Pearlescent Dye]]
XP: 51-55 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 37-40
[Metadata]
ID: ENDERMITE_37; ENDERMITE_40
Endermites are small [[Mob|Mobs]] similar to [[Silverfish (disambiguation)|Silverfish]] found in [[The End]]. They are spawned by [[Seer|Seers]] and can also spawn when a [[Seer]] dies. They are also sometimes spawned by breaking [[End Stone]] with anything other than a [[Stonk]].
## Spawning
Lower level Endermites have a chance to spawn when breaking [[End Stone]] with anything other than a [[Stonk]].
Higher level Endermites can be spawned by [[Seer|Seers]] as projectiles.

# Experience
Aliases: EXP, Experience Level, Experience Orb, Experience Orbs, XP
Categories: Vanilla objects, Mechanics
Experience is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Experience.
[Experience]
Experience, also know as EXP or XP, is a mechanic used for [[Enchanting]] and the [[Experiments]].
## Usage
Experience Levels can be used to upgrade items with the [[Enchantment Table]], as well as complete [[Experiments|Superpairs]] and purchase daily resets for the [[Experimentation Table]].
Experience Levels can also be used with [[Ultimate Enchantments|Ultimate Enchantment]] [[Wisdom (Enchantment)|Wisdom]] to increase [[Intelligence]].
## Obtaining
There are many ways to obtain experience such as:
- Splashing [[Experience Bottle|Experience Bottles]], [[Grand Experience Bottle|Grand Experience Bottles]], [[Titanic Experience Bottle|Titanic Experience Bottles]], or [[Colossal Experience Bottle|Colossal Experience Bottles]]
- Killing [[Mob|Mobs]]
- Breaking [[End Stone]] with the [[Endermite Pet]] / [[Stonk]]
- Breaking logs with the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Ocelot Pet]]
- Collecting loot from [[Fishing]]
- Mining [[Ores]]
- Smelting items in [[Furnace|Furnaces]]
## Ways to Increase Gain
|Type|Description|
|-|-|
|Enchanting Skill ([[Enchanting]])|Gives [[Experience]] +5% Experience per level with a maximum of +300% at Enchanting LX (60) ([[Enchanting]]).|
|Experience Potions ([[Experience Potion]])|Gives [[Experience]] +5% Experience per potion level with a maximum of +20%.|
|[[Experience Artifact]]|Gives +25% XP.|
|[[Lapis Armor]]|Gives [[Experience]] +50-200% Experience from mined ores.|
|[[Shimmering Light Armor]]|Increases all [[Experience]] Experience orbs gained from monsters and ores by +200-300%.|
|[[Enchanted Book]] (Experience ([[Experience (Enchantment)]]))|Grants a +12.5-62.5% chance for mobs and ores to drop double experience.|
|[[Enchanted Book]] ([[Magnet]])|Grants an additional [[Experience]] 1-6 Experience orb(s) every time you successfully catch a fish.|
|[[Enchanted Book]] ([[No Pain No Gain]])|Grants a +20-100% chance to gain [[Experience]] 10 Experience every time you take hits from mobs.|
|[[Bitter Iced Tea]]|Buffs the [[Mana]] and Experience values of potions by +20%.|
|[[Viking's Tear]]|Adds [[Experience]] +20% Experience orbs, 10 [[Combat Wisdom]], and +10 [[Health]] per second.|
|Magnetic ([[Reforging]]) ( [[Lapis Crystal]]) Reforge Bonus|Earn [[Experience]] +100% Experience when mining.|
|Bestiary Tiers ([[Bestiary]])|For every level of a [[Mob]] collection, you will unlock +20% chance for extra [[Experience]] Experience orbs.|
## Trivia
- While in the [[Catacombs|Catacombs Dungeon]], your experience bar instead represents your selected [[Classes|Class]]'s ultimate ability cooldown.
- The experience required for each level is determined using Minecraft 1.8+ values.

|Jun 17, 2019|Fixed breaking [[Ores]] with [[Silk Touch]] giving experience orbs.|

# Eye of Ender
Aliases: Eye Of Ender
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Ender
Eye of Ender is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Eye of Ender.
This item's ability is currently disabled!
[Eye of Ender]
Type: Enchanted item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
Uses: [[Furniture]] material
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 15 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EYE_OF_ENDER
Tooltip: COMMON
Eye of Ender is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] vanilla item. It can be used as a [[Furniture]] material. An enchanted version of this item is unlocked at [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearl VI (6)]]; [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Ender Pearl]] 1x [[Blaze Powder]]|Recipe: [[Blaze Powder]] + [[Ender Pearl]] → Eye of Ender|
## Usage
### Carpentry Ingredient
[[Carpentry]] items are listed below. All carpentry items require the listed items and [[Carpentry]] level to craft and use.
|Icon|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- The Eye of Ender can be used with [[Glitched Items|End Portal Frames]], and in the correct placement, there will be an End Portal that doesn't transport you anywhere. This can be used with a [[Portals|Portal to The End]] placed below it (having broken the frame) and it will function similar to a vanilla end portal, as it will take you to the SkyBlock [[The End|End]].
- The right-click function has been disabled as of January 11, 2022 due to spamming multiple of these would cause lag and possible server crashes.

# Farming Tools
Aliases: Farming Axe, Farming Tool, Farming Tool Level, Farming axe, Farming tool, Farming tools, Specialized Farming Tool, Specialized Farming Tools
Categories: Vanilla objects
[Farming Tools]
Farming Tools are used in [[Farming]] to harvest crops.
## Basic Farming Tools

## Specialized Farming Tools
Specialized Farming Tools specialize in one [[Crops (disambiguation)|Crop]]. One of each Specialized Farming Tool can be stored in the [[Farming Toolkit]].
### List
|Icon|Name|Crop|Rarity|Ability|Source|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Euclid's Wheat Sickle]]|[[Euclid's Wheat Sickle]]|[[Wheat]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Gauss Carrot Shovel]]|[[Gauss Carrot Shovel]]|[[Carrot]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Pythagorean Potato Shovel]]|[[Pythagorean Potato Shovel]]|[[Potato]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Pumpkin Dicer]]|[[Pumpkin Dicer]]|[[Pumpkin]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]]|[[Turing Sugar Cane Cutter]]|[[Sugar Cane]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Melon Dicer]]|[[Melon Dicer]]|[[Melon Slice]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Cactus Knife]]|[[Cactus Knife]]|[[Cactus]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Cocoa Chopper]]|[[Cocoa Chopper]]|[[Cocoa Beans]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Fungi Cutter]]|[[Fungi Cutter]]|[[Mushroom]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]]|[[Newton Nether Wart Cutter]]|[[Nether Wart]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Eclipse Sickle]]|[[Eclipse Sickle]]|[[Sunflower]]; [[Moonflower]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
|[[Wild Rose Cutter]]|[[Wild Rose Cutter]]|[[Wild Rose]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|+4-200 [[Crop Fortune]]; +1-3 [[Farming Wisdom]]|SkyMart ([[The Garden]]); (250 [[Copper]])|
### Mechanics
Specialized Farming Tools increase in level by breaking a mature crop matching the type of crop the tool is designed for. Levels increase the [[Crop Fortune]], [[Farming Wisdom]], and [[Gemstone]] slots of the Tool. Each following level requires more experience than the proceeding level.
After a Tool reaches level 40, [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] are required to unlock the next levels. A maximum of 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be used to get a max tool level of 50. All 10 [[Overclocker 3000|Overclocker 3000s]] can be applied without having reached the next required level.
After a Tool reaches level 50, every 200k experience will gain an internal level, but not increase its stats or display. Instead, each level drops a [[Tool Exp Capsule]]. Every level after 50 requires the same amount of experiences.
### Experience Gain
The amount of experience a Tool gains for each crop broken is equal to the amount of collection the player gained divided by a crop-specific divisor, and further increased by the [[Garden Chips|Mechamind Chip]]. It is not affected by [[Farming|Farming XP]] gain.
In the table below, the base drop rate is provided solely for comparison purposes, and the Mechamind bonus is not included.
|Crop|Base Drop Rate|Divisor|Effective XP per Crop Broken|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wheat]] + [[Seeds]]|2.5 (1 Wheat + 1.5 Seeds)|1|2.50|
|[[Carrot]]|3|3.5|0.86|
|[[Potato]]|3|3|1.00|
|[[Melon Slice]]|5|4|1.25|
|[[Sugar Cane]]|2|2|1.00|
|[[Pumpkin]]|1|0.85|1.18|
|[[Cactus]]|2|1.5|1.33|
|[[Cocoa Beans]]|3|1.5|2.00|
|[[Mushroom]]|1|0.95|1.05|
|[[Nether Wart]]|2.5|3|0.83|
|[[Sunflower]]|2|2|1.00|
|[[Moonflower]]|2|2|1.00|
|[[Wild Rose]]|2|2.5|0.80|
or follow by this formula: Tool Xp Gained =(Collection GainedCrop Divisor×Mechamind Chip Multiplier)

## Vanilla Farming Tools
Farming Tools is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Hoe.
All existing Vanilla Farming Tools are Vanilla Hoes. They do not grant any [[Farming Fortune]] and generally don't serve any purpose. The wooden version is used in various farming [[Minions|Minion]] recipes.
|Icon|Type|Recipe|Rarity|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wooden Hoe]]|Wooden|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Planks]] ×2 + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|[[Golden Hoe]]|Golden|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Gold Ingot]] ×2 + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|[[Stone Hoe]]|Stone|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] ×2 + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|[[Iron Hoe]]|Iron|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×2 + [[Stick]] ×2|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|[[Diamond Hoe]]|Diamond|▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Diamond]] ×2 + [[Stick]] ×2|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|
## History

|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Created Farming Tool Type.|
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Recategorized all Hoes and Axes used for farming.|
|Apr 21, 2026|Allowed for Specialized Farming Tools to be stored in the [[Farming Toolkit]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Created Farming Tool Type.|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Recategorized all Hoes and Axes used for farming.|

## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Fern can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 4 [[Coins]].
### Trades
One Fern can be obtained in exchange for 8 [[Dirt]] in the [[Trades]] menu, after unlocking [[Seeds|Seeds IV (4)]].
## Usage
### Private Island
A Fern can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]]. It will not drop itself when broken, even with [[Shears]] or [[Silk Touch]].

# Flight
Aliases: Double Jump, Double jump, Flying
Categories: Vanilla objects, More info needed
Flight is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Flying.
Flight is a mechanic that allows players to fly as if in Creative or Spectator mode.
## Private Islands
On the [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]], flight can be achieved by one of the following sources:
- [[Booster Cookie]]: Allows the player to fly for its duration.
- [[Phoenix Pet]]: Allows the player to fly while the pet is equipped.
- [[Magical Mushroom Soup]]: When consumed, increases flight duration by 2 minutes, which stacks up to [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Max flight duration from mushroom soup; When this info is added, remove this.). This duration is paused if the aforementioned sources are active, or while not on the [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]].
  - [[Mystical Mushroom Soup]]: Similar to Magical Mushroom Soup, but increases the duration by 200 minutes instead of 2.
All of these methods also stop the player from taking [[Damage Calculation|Fall Damage]] on [[Private Island]] and [[The Garden]].
## Public Islands
On public islands, certain areas and abilities allow the player to fly:
- [[Wizard Brawl]]
- [[Spirit Boots]]
- [[Ghosts]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] and [[Kuudra's Hollow]]
- The following mobs can trigger some form of levitation:
  - [[Hideonleaf]]
  - [[Hideonsun]]
  - [[Murkbat]]
  - [[Hivethief]]
- [[Abiphone Contacts Trio]] levitates the player for a short duration.
- [[Bat Person Armor]] removes the cooldown from [[Grappling Hook]] and increase its velocity, which allows for movement in the air.
### Double Jumping
Certain abilities allow the player to double jump, emulating flight:
- [[Spider's Boots]]
- [[Tarantula Boots]]
- [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Boots]]
- [[Frog's Spirit]]
Linked from: [[Consumables]], [[Fly (disambiguation)]]

# Flowers
Aliases: Allium, Azure Bluet, Blue Orchid, Dandelion, Flower, Lilac, Orange Tulip, Oxeye Daisy, Peony, Pink Tulip, Poppy, Red Tulip, Rose Bush, White Tulip
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Blocks, Crafting ingredients, Flowers, Machine Fuels
Flowers is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Flowers.
[Flowers]
Type: Block
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Block Details]
Location: [[Flower House]]
Skill ([[Skills]]) XP Given: +1 Foraging XP ([[Foraging]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Flower Sack]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: YELLOW_FLOWER (Dandelion); RED_ROSE (Poppy); RED_ROSE:1 (Blue Orchid); RED_ROSE:2 (Allium); RED_ROSE:3 (Azure Bluet); RED_ROSE:4 (Red Tulip); RED_ROSE:5 (Orange Tulip); RED_ROSE:6 (White Tulip); RED_ROSE:7 (Pink Tulip); RED_ROSE:8 (Oxeye Daisy); DOUBLE_PLANT (Sunflower); DOUBLE_PLANT:1 (Lilac); DOUBLE_PLANT:4 (Rose Bush); DOUBLE_PLANT:5 (Peony)
Tooltip: COMMON
Flowers are [[Rarity|COMMON]] decorative blocks that come in a variety of colors, and can be placed on [[Grass]] blocks or in flower pots. They also have some practical uses.
## Obtaining
### Natural Harvesting
Flowers of various types can be found in the [[Village]] outside the [[Flower House]], and in [[The Park]]. They can also be spawned on a player's [[Private Island]] by using [[Bone Meal]] on an area of grass blocks, which will spawn flowers appropriate to their [[Biome Stick|Biome]].
Flower drops are not boosted by [[Farming Fortune]] or [[Foraging Fortune]]. Harvesting a flower grants +1 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]].
### Flower Minions
Most flowers can be obtained from [[Flower Minion|Flower Minions]], which are obtainable from the [[Dark Auction]].
### Builder
Most flowers can be purchased from the [[Builder]], except for the [[Sunflower]], which can only be obtained from the [[Sunflower Minion]], [[The Garden]], or through [[Trading]].
## Types
|Dandelion|Poppy|Blue Orchid|Azure Bluet|Oxeye Daisy|
|-|-|-|-|-|
[Flowers]
|Allium|Lilac|Rose Bush|White Tulip|Pink Tulip|
[Flowers]
|Red Tulip|Orange Tulip|Sunflower|||
|[[Flowers]]|[[Flowers]]|[[Sunflower]]|||

### Private Island
Flowers can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].

A Furnace is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] block that can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] and used to smelt various items.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8x [[Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] ×8 → Furnace|
## Usage
### Placing and Smelting
A Furnace can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]]. It can then be used to smelt items.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Minecart with Furnace]]|None|[[Furnace]] 1x Furnace 1x [[Minecart]]|Recipe: Furnace + [[Minecart]] → [[Minecart with Furnace]]|

|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- Furnaces used to be able to use SkyBlock items such as [[Necron's Handle|Necron's Handles]] as fuel, due to these items having a vanilla item model that is normally fuel.

# Glass

Glass is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Glass.
[Glass]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLASS
Tooltip: COMMON
Glass is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] Block that can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Glass can be purchased from the [[Builder]] for 4 [[Coins]].
### Smelting Touch
Glass can be obtained by mining [[Sand]] with a tool with the [[Smelting Touch]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]].
### Minions
Glass can be obtained from [[Sand Minion|Sand Minions]] with the [[Auto Smelter]] upgrade applied.
### Smelting
|Ingredients|Recipe|
|-|-|
|1x [[Sand]]|Recipe: [[Sand]] + [[Coal]] → Glass|
## Usage
### Private Island
Glass can be placed as a block on the player's [[Private Island]] or plots in [[The Garden]].

# Glass Bottle
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients
Glass Bottle is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Glass Bottle.
[Glass Bottle]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLASS_BOTTLE
Tooltip: COMMON
A Glass Bottle is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item that can be turned into a [[Water Bottle]] or used to craft various items.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
8 Glass Bottles can be purchased from the [[Alchemist]] for 48 [[Coins]], which averages to 6 [[Coins]] each.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|3x [[Glass]]|Recipe: [[Glass]] ×3 → Glass Bottle x3|
### Water Bottles
Drinking from a [[Water Bottle]] will turn it back into a Glass Bottle.
## Usage
### Water Bottles
An Empty Bottle can be converted into a [[Water Bottle]] by right clicking it on a water source.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Titanic Experience Bottle]]|Lapis Lazuli VIII (8) ([[Lapis Lazuli]])|[[Glass Bottle]] 1x Glass Bottle 6x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] ×6 + Glass Bottle → [[Titanic Experience Bottle]]|
|[[Grand Experience Bottle]]|Lapis Lazuli V (5) ([[Lapis Lazuli]])|[[Glass Bottle]] 1x Glass Bottle 6x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]] ×6 + Glass Bottle → [[Grand Experience Bottle]]|
|[[Experience Bottle]]|Lapis Lazuli II (2) ([[Lapis Lazuli]])|[[Glass Bottle]] 1x Glass Bottle 6x [[Lapis Lazuli]]|Recipe: [[Lapis Lazuli]] ×6 + Glass Bottle → [[Experience Bottle]]|
|[[Empty Thunder Bottle]]|None|[[Glass Bottle]] 1x Glass Bottle 4x [[Orb of Energy]]|Recipe: [[Orb of Energy]] ×4 + Glass Bottle → [[Empty Thunder Bottle]]|
|[[Celestial Mason Jar]]|None|[[Glass Bottle]] 1x Glass Bottle 4x [[Feast Flask]]|Recipe: [[Feast Flask]] ×4 + Glass Bottle → [[Celestial Mason Jar]]|
|[[Overpriced Drink]]|None|[[Glass Bottle]] 1x Glass Bottle 8x [[Aggourdian]] 8x [[Designer Coffee Beans]]|Recipe: [[Aggourdian]] x8 + [[Designer Coffee Beans]] x8 + Glass Bottle → [[Overpriced Drink]]|
|[[Trunk Polish]]|None|[[Glass Bottle]] 1x Glass Bottle 4x [[Creambloom]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Melon Juice]]|Recipe: [[Melon Juice]] x32 ×4 + [[Creambloom]] ×4 + Glass Bottle → [[Trunk Polish]]|
|[[Melon Juice Mixin]]|None|[[Glass Bottle]] 1x Glass Bottle 2x [[Soggybud]] 16x [[Melon Juice]]|Recipe: [[Melon Juice]] x16 + [[Soggybud]] x2 + Glass Bottle → [[Melon Juice Mixin]]|

Glistering Melon Slice is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Potions|Brewing]] ingredient used in making Healing [[Potions]]. The potions made can be upgraded with [[Glowstone]] to increase the strength, [[Redstone Dust]] to increase the duration, and [[Gunpowder]] to make it a splash potion.

## Obtaining
### Mining
Glowstone Dust can be mined on the [[Crimson Isle]].
#### Ticks Thresholds

### Shop Purchase
Players can also obtain Glowstone Dust by buying [[Glowstone]] from [[Builder]], placing it and breaking it on the [[Private Island]]. While this doesn't give collection, it can help players on [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] to obtain [[Magical Mushroom Soup]], or upgrade potions.
### Minions
Glowstone Dust can be produced by [[Glowstone Minion]], or [[Inferno Minion]] with [[Inferno Minion Fuel]].
## Usage
### Brewing
[[Potions|Brewing]] [[Potions]] with Glowstone Dust amplifies a potion's level by 1.
### Crafting

|[[Glowstone]]|None|[[Glowstone Dust]] 4x Glowstone Dust|Recipe: Glowstone Dust ×4 → [[Glowstone]]|

|Glowstone Minion II ([[Glowstone Minion]])|Glowstone Dust I (1)|1x Glowstone Minion I ([[Glowstone Minion]]) [[Glowstone Dust]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Glowstone Dust|Recipe: Glowstone Dust x32 ×8 + [[Glowstone Minion]] → [[Glowstone Minion]]|

# Gold Ingot
Aliases: Dwarven Gold, Gold, Gold Collection, Gold collection, Pure Gold
Categories: Vanilla objects, Common items, Items in Sacks, Items, Pages with UIs, Mob Drops, Craftable items, Pages with broken file links, Crafting ingredients, Templates with deprecated argument names, Brewing ingredients, Gold, Collections, Gold Ingot Collection
Gold Ingot is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Gold Ingot.
[Gold Ingot]
Type: Collection item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Beginner Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]])
[Shop]
Merchants: Mine Merchant ([[Mining Merchant]]) [[Gold Forger]]
Buy: 6 [[Coins]]; 5.5 [[Coins]]
Sell: 4 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOLD_INGOT
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient Collection Item; COMMON
Gold Ingot is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] resource obtained from [[Mining]] [[Ores|Gold Ore]] at the [[Gold Mine]], [[Gunpowder Mines]], or mining Pure Gold at the [[Dwarven Mines]] or [[Crystal Hollows]]. It can also be obtained from [[Gold Minion|Gold Minions]], or bought from NPCs for 6 [[Coins]] from the [[Mining Merchant|Mine Merchant]]; or for 5.5 [[Coins]] from the [[Gold Forger]].
## Collection
Collecting gold ingots increases the player's gold ingot [[Collections|collection]], which grants useful items and perks upon collecting certain amounts of gold ingots.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Gold Minion]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Cleaver]] Recipe|+4|
|III|250|[[Looting]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|IV|500|Portal to the Gold Mine ([[Portals]]) Recipe|+4|
|V|1,000|[[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|2,500|[[Absorption Potion]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|5,000|[[Scavenger]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|VIII|10,000|[[Enchanted Gold Block]] Recipe|+4|
|IX|25,000|[[Fortune]] Exp Discount (-25%)|+4|
|X|500,000|[[Enchanted Clock]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Mining
Mining [[Ores|Gold Ore]] using a [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] with [[Smelting Touch]] will drop a Gold Ingot. [[Ores|Gold Ore]] can be found and mined in the [[Gold Mine]] and the [[Gunpowder Mines]].
#### Pure Gold
Pure Gold will always drop Gold Ingots even without [[Smelting Touch]]. A [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] with a [[Breaking Power]] of 3 or better is required to mine it.
Pure Gold is located both in [[Dwarven Mines]]' [[Royal Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]]'s [[Mines of Divan]]. It drops [[Gold Ingot]] 5 Gold Ingots. It is affected by [[Compact]] and [[Mining Spread]].
It takes the appearance of [[Block of Gold]].
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv65]|[[Golden Ghoul]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv65]|[[Golden Ghoul]]|9|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|35%|
|[Lv88-93]|[[Golden Ghoul (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv88-93]|[[Golden Ghoul (Stranded)]]|9|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|35%|
|[Lv88]|[[Zombie Villager (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv88]|[[Zombie Villager (Stranded)]]|9|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|35%|
|[Lv93]|[[Zombie Villager (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv93]|[[Zombie Villager (Stranded)]]|9|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|35%|
|[Lv8]|[[Redstone Pigman (Stranded)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
### Minions
A [[Gold Minion]] with the [[Auto Smelter]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]] will produce Gold Ingots.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Block of Gold]]|Recipe: [[Block of Gold]] → Gold Ingot x9|
|None|9x [[Gold Nugget]]|Recipe: [[Gold Nugget]] ×9 → Gold Ingot|
### Smelting
Recipe: [[Ores]] + [[Coal]] → Gold Ingot
### Stranded Mode
Gold can be obtained by killing A sprite icon representing Pigman in Hypixel SkyBlock [[Pigman]] on your [[Private Island|Island]].

### Brewing
Gold Ingots can be used to brew [[Absorption Potion|Absorption Potions]].
### Bank Upgrades
While not strictly included within the Gold Ingot Collection, upgrading your [[Bank|Bank Account]] requires progress in the collection.
Bank Upgrades:
|Account|Upgrade Requirements Cost|Upgrade Requirements Gold Ingot Collection|Upgrade Requirements [[Museum]] Rank|Max Balance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Starter||||50M [[Coins]]|
|Gold|[[Enchanted Gold Block]] + 5M [[Coins]]|100K Gold Ingot Collection||100M [[Coins]]|
|Deluxe|[[Enchanted Gold Block]] x5 + 10M [[Coins]]|250K Gold Ingot Collection|Wealthy|250M [[Coins]]|
|Super Deluxe|[[Enchanted Gold Block]] x20 + 25M [[Coins]]|500K Gold Ingot Collection|Prosperous|500M [[Coins]]|
|Premier|[[Enchanted Gold Block]] x50 + 50M [[Coins]]|1M Gold Ingot Collection|Gilded|1B [[Coins]]|

|Palatial|[[Enchanted Gold Block]] x200 + 200M [[Coins]]|3M Gold Ingot Collection|Palatial|60B [[Coins]]|
### [[The Alchemist's Staff]] requires giving [[Gold Ingot]] 64 (1 stack) Gold Ingot to [[Baar]] or the [[Alchemage]].
### Golden Dragon Pet
The Shining Scales ability of [[Golden Dragon Pet]] provides 11.1 [[Strength]] and 2.2 [[Magic Find]] per every digit in the Gold Ingot collection, up to 100 [[Strength]] and 20 [[Magic Find]] as 100,000,000 is limit to the ability.
